### Qwen Code

| 字段 | 记录 |
| --- | --- |
| 矩阵结论 | `/hooks` · command/HTTP/prompt · 默认并行，`sequential: true` 强制顺序（同一事件内按 Project、User、System、Extension 排），`async: true` 占 10 个并发槽 · 退出码 2 阻断，其他非零、HTTP 非 2xx 与超时放行；无 `onFailure` 一类失败开关 · headless 下调用命令或 Skill 的输入在展开前先过 `UserPromptSubmit`（v0.25.3 起稳定通道包含）· v0.27.0 起新增 `FileChanged` 事件（事件表 22→23 个）与 `hookSpecificOutput.watchPaths` 动态注册：静态注册只认 cwd 内的字面文件名、不展开 glob 也不递归，动态注册接受绝对文件与递归目录并跳过 `.git`/`node_modules`，写入稳定后（源码 `awaitWriteFinish` 为 200 毫秒阈值、50 毫秒轮询）才报事件，观察型不能否决写入也不能加模型上下文；SSH 执行环境、shell sandbox worker 与 managed dispatcher 不启动本地 watcher · 条件：`experimental.mods` 开启后插件 `hooks/hooks.json` 的 `modules` 注册隔离 QuickJS 函数模块（mod，Claude Code Mod API 子集），交互终端与 headless 注册 Slash 命令、拦截生命周期并写 transcript 日志，`tool.call`/`tool.check` 工具拦截仅 headless（v0.25.3 起） |
| 入口与配置 | `/hooks` 查看和管理已加载 Hook；设置文件和 Extension 都可声明。实验性 mod 经 `qwen extensions install <dir> --consent` 安装，在 `experimental.mods` 开启且工作区受信任后加载。 |
| 文件与目录 | 用户与项目 `settings.json`；Extension 可内联 Hooks、引用文件或使用默认 `hooks/hooks.json`。 |
| 具体行为 | 覆盖提示、模型、工具、权限、会话、压缩、Subagent、通知等生命周期，并能阻断或返回修改后的控制结果。command Hook 的退出码表逐字为：`0` 是成功、由 stdout 里的 JSON 对象控制行为，其他 stdout（包括 `42` 这类裸 JSON 值）按纯文本处理——在 `SessionStart`、`UserPromptSubmit`、`UserPromptExpansion` 上加进模型上下文，在其余事件上保留为 system 消息，而看起来像 JSON 对象却解析失败的输出永不加进模型上下文；`2` 是阻断错误，忽略 stdout 并把 stderr 作为错误反馈交给模型；其他退出码是非阻断错误，stderr 只在 debug 模式下显示、执行继续。HTTP Hook 的 3xx 响应按其他非 2xx 状态一样处理，即一次非阻断的 Hook 失败，且绝不联系重定向目标。执行顺序与并发（2026-10-28 复核，文档固定在提交 `1dc74d58384ca1fe8c29730970f1f7c60d1e5265`，PR #14129，2026-10-25T14:50:26Z，96,533 字节）：默认并行——“By default, hooks execute in parallel for better performance”，需要顺序时在 Hook 定义里写 `sequential: true`（“Use `sequential: true` in hook definition to enforce order-dependent execution”），顺序执行的 Hook 可以把改过的输入传给链上的下一条（“Sequential hooks can modify input for subsequent hooks in the chain”）；并行时所有 Hook 收到同一份输入、“the last replacement in configuration order wins, and an invalid replacement from any of them stops the call”，顺序执行时每条拿到上一条的替换结果作为 `tool_input`。顺序执行时的来源次序另有明确规定，`## Hook Configuration` 一节逐字为 “Within one event, sequential hooks from settings and extensions run in this order: Project, User, System, Extension.”，同一句前还写 “Hooks in the system settings files (System and SystemDefaults) load with the source System and, like user hooks, regardless of folder trust.”。工具批次另有两条规则：“When several calls from one turn are scheduled together, as in the interactive terminal UI, every call's hooks run before any of them runs.” 与 “A shell command that a hook could still rewrite is not run in parallel with other tool calls.”；`MessageDisplay` 一类中途 Hook 是 “At most one mid-stream hook execution per message is in flight at a time; while one runs, newer debounced payloads _replace_ the queued one rather than piling up behind it.”。事件表在该坐标下列出 22 个事件（2026-11-08 复核标签 v0.27.0、提交 `03b0c2435be1890a21b117860eaf389076787275` 下的同一文档已增至 105,235 字节、23 个事件，新增的是 `FileChanged`）；相对此前记录的提交 `52ae53b02a17d6214e9e4f81578f2ba2725539a6`（96,225 字节），该文档唯一的改动是 `UserPromptSubmit` 的 `additionalContext` 去重句 “If the sanitized payload is byte-identical to a block a hook already delivered earlier in the same session, it is not appended again, so a deterministic standing instruction reaches the model once rather than once per turn; this is unconditional with no opt-out, and any change to the payload re-injects it.”，与并发无关。**文件变更观察**（2026-11-08 复核，标签 v0.27.0、提交 `03b0c2435be1890a21b117860eaf389076787275`）：v0.27.0 新增第 23 个事件 `FileChanged`，事件表逐字为 “\| `FileChanged` \| After an explicitly watched file is added, modified, or deleted \| Basename of the changed file \|”，专节 `#### FileChanged` 的 Purpose 逐字为 “Observes additions, modifications, and deletions of explicitly watched files in local, non-managed sessions. Changes may come from Qwen Code, shell commands, editors, or other processes. The common input fields identify the listening session, including when another session writes a shared directory.”，专页 `docs/users/features/file-changed-hooks.md`（7,621 字节）另写 “Only file events are delivered. Directory-self `addDir` and `unlinkDir` events are not delivered. Integrations tracking a watched root's own lifecycle must check filesystem state (for example with `stat`) and reconcile independently; a child's `unlink` does not prove that the root was removed.” 与 “The common `session_id` and `cwd` belong to the listening session; they do not identify the writer. Multiple sessions watching a shared directory can each receive its changes. File content is not included.”。事件字段是 `{ "file_path": "/home/me/work/demo/.envrc", "event": "change" }`，`file_path` 为绝对路径、`event` 取 `add`／`change`／`unlink`；Matcher 一栏逐字为 “Filters against the file basename using the existing matcher rules. Literal cwd filenames separated by `\|` also register static paths. Omitted or empty matchers, `*`, and `.*` register no static paths; they receive events from the session's watched paths, including dynamically registered paths.”，`### Matcher Patterns` 表把它归到新的 “File Events” 一行并标 “✅ Regex”，表后专段逐字为 “For `FileChanged`, registering static paths uses literal filenames in cwd; filtering events uses the basename and the rules below.”，即**注册与过滤是两件事**。该事件不能阻断：专节写 “Hook output cannot veto or undo a write or add model context.”，专页写 “`FileChanged` cannot veto or undo a write or add model context.” 与 “Watcher or hook failures are logged and leave the originating file operation unchanged.”。输出只有 `watchPaths` 一个键，专节逐字为 “Successful synchronous `hookSpecificOutput.watchPaths`, with `hookEventName: "FileChanged"`, replaces the dynamic watch list with absolute files or recursive directories. The last valid, explicit list in hook execution order wins; `[]` clears it, while omitted or invalid lists leave it unchanged. Async output does not register paths. Static registrations remain active.”；`SessionStart` 侧新增的同一键逐字为 “`hookSpecificOutput.watchPaths`: absolute files or recursive directories to watch for `FileChanged`, from synchronous output with `hookEventName: "SessionStart"`.”，专页补上 “A successful synchronous `SessionStart` hook can register absolute files or directories through `hookSpecificOutput.watchPaths`. Directories are watched recursively, including children created later. Recursive discovery skips `.git` and `node_modules` subtrees. To observe a path inside them, register that file or directory explicitly. Special file types are ignored, and ordinary directories have no depth limit. Initial registration waits for watcher readiness before `SessionStart` returns.”。静态注册的边界逐字为 “Static roots accept only bare literal filenames, including dotfiles. Regex/glob metacharacters and path separators do not create static roots. Existing directories and paths that cannot be inspected are excluded; missing files may be watched for creation. Static file discovery observes their parent directory while excluding unrelated entries before traversal. Use explicit `watchPaths` for recursive directories. Changing the session working directory relocates static roots before relocation finishes; explicit absolute dynamic roots stay unchanged.” 与 “Static registration does not expand globs or scan directories recursively.”；动态清单的替换语义表四行为 “A list of absolute files or directories \| Replaces the previous dynamic list”、“`[]` \| Clears the dynamic list”、“Omitted or invalid `watchPaths` \| Keeps the previous dynamic list”、“Output from an async hook \| Does not register or change paths”，并要求 “All entries must be absolute paths; one invalid entry makes the list invalid.”。观察限制逐字为 “The initial directory scan is not replayed. Native filesystem events from a recent write may arrive after registration. Explicitly registered symbolic-link files and directory roots observe their targets, including links in ancestor paths. Notifications use the configured alias; registering multiple aliases to one target produces a notification for each alias. Refresh registration after retargeting a link. Symbolic links discovered inside a recursive root are not traversed. Events are reported after writes settle.” 与 “Watching is best effort: filesystem coalescing, inaccessible paths, process downtime, and path reconfiguration can leave gaps. Integrations that require a complete synchronized copy must handle initial scans, reconciliation, and retry.”；原子写临时文件按 “Recursive discovery ignores atomic-write temporary files named `<target>.<12 lowercase hex characters>.tmp`, including delayed renames and orphans. Explicitly registering such a file still observes it.” 处理。官方给的示例用途是把 `/memory` 显示的记忆目录注册进观察清单，并提醒 “Choose a log or synchronization destination outside the watched paths to avoid triggering the handler with its own writes.” 与 “A hook that writes a watched file can trigger itself; avoid those writes or deduplicate the work.”；专页同时写 “Only explicitly registered paths are watched: memory folders are not registered automatically, and there is no dedicated auto-memory enable/disable event.”。 |
| 作用域与优先级 | 用户、可信项目与 Extension 来源；项目 Hook 随仓库共享。 |
| 扩展构成 | 公开文档包括 command、HTTP 与 prompt；运行时还存在 session-only 的内部 function Hook（由 Skill 系统使用，与下面的 mod 不同）。自 v0.25.3 起另有实验性 Mod API：`experimental.mods: true` 开启后，受信任工作区里启用的 Claude 或原生 Qwen 扩展可在 `hooks/hooks.json` 顶层用 `modules`（官方示例 `{ "modules": ["./register.js"] }`，路径相对该文件且必须在扩展内部）声明一个 ESM 入口（单个 `.js`/`.mjs`/`.cjs`/`.ts`/`.mts`/`.cts`），导出 `register(on, options)` 注册函数 Hook，官方称之为 mod 并写明这是 “a small subset of the Claude Code Mod API”、“not full compatibility with existing Claude Mods”。mod 运行在「每插件一个、位于会话 worker 内的隔离 QuickJS 环境」，没有 Node、文件系统与网络 API，并有内存、栈、源码/输出与执行时长上限；它能用 `$.command.register({ name, description, argumentHint })` 在 `session.start` 期间注册不经模型请求运行的 Slash 命令，用 `on(<event>, ...)` 拦截 `session.start`/`session.end`/`command.run`/`command.register`/`ui.log`/`tool.call`/`tool.check` 七类事件（事件名支持精确名、`command.*` 一类前缀、`*` 与单个 `!session.*` 否定，`!*` 与 `!!...` 被拒绝），用 `$.ui.log` 写 transcript（text 模式走 stderr、JSON 模式走 `system/ui_log`，都不进模型输入，每条上限 2,000 字符），模块变量在 stream-json 或交互进程里跨回合保留、`/clear` 与 `/resume` 不重载模块，而 `state`/`store`、渲染、流式事件、热重载、JSX/TSX、运行时 package import、内置命令拦截、工具注册与 `$.tool` API 都未实现。工具拦截（`tool.call` 返回 `{deny}` 或 `{result}`、`tool.check` 的 `next` 返回带 `decision`（`allow`/`ask`/`deny`）的对象）只在 headless 会话可用；交互终端（Ink 与 OpenTUI）只支持命令、生命周期与日志，显式 `tool.call`/`tool.check`/`tool.*` 注册会在模型请求前拒绝初始化。`pluginConfigs.<mod>.toolDialect` 取 `claude` 或 `native`（默认）切换工具名方言（`run_shell_command`↔`Bash`、`edit`↔`Edit`、`write_file`↔`Write`），`pluginConfigs.<mod>.options` 对应 manifest 的 `userConfig`，选项按 SystemDefaults → User → System 合并、工作区设置即使受信任也忽略。执行控制字段有两个：`sequential: true` 让同一事件的多条 Hook 按配置顺序执行（默认并行），`async: true` 只有 command 类型支持、让该 Hook 在后台运行且不阻塞主流程（“Only `command` type supports asynchronous execution. Setting `"async": true` runs the hook in the background without blocking the main flow.”）。事件输出侧自 v0.27.0 起多一个跨事件共用的注册键 `hookSpecificOutput.watchPaths`：`SessionStart` 与 `FileChanged` 两个事件的**同步**输出都能返回它，值是绝对文件或目录的数组、整体替换上一次的动态观察清单，`[]` 清空、省略或非法则保持原样、异步 Hook 的输出不注册路径，多个 Hook 都返回时 “the last valid, explicitly supplied list in hook execution order wins”，且 “All entries must be absolute paths; one invalid entry makes the list invalid.”，静态文件名注册在动态清单被替换或清空后仍然有效。`FileChanged` 自己的 `hookSpecificOutput` 只有这一个键——它没有 `PreToolUse` 的 `permissionDecision`、也没有 `UserPromptSubmit` 的 `additionalContext`，官方专页明确写它 “cannot veto or undo a write or add model context”。底层实现是 `packages/core/src/hooks/file-changed-watcher.ts`（9,585 字节）里的 chokidar watcher，`watch()` 选项为 `ignoreInitial: true`、`followSymlinks: false` 与 `awaitWriteFinish: { stabilityThreshold: 200, pollInterval: 50 }`，`ignored` 回调跳过既非文件也非目录的 stat、匹配 `/\.[0-9a-f]{12}\.tmp$/` 的原子写临时文件（显式注册的同名文件除外）以及动态根内路径分量含 `.git` 或 `node_modules` 的条目，并且**没有传 `depth` 选项**，与文档 “ordinary directories have no depth limit” 一致；调试通道是 `createDebugLogger('FILE_CHANGED_WATCHER')`。 |
| 加载与刷新 | 启动时合并配置；Extension 热重载与 Hook 管理入口可更新当前运行时状态。 |
| 适用界面 | 以 Qwen Code CLI 为准；Headless、ACP 和 IDE Companion 中不同的加载行为会单独注明。 |
| 权限与信任 | 项目 Hook 只在可信文件夹加载；Hook 自身的命令和网络访问需要按可执行配置审查。 |
| 条件与边界 | 内部 function Hook 不是普通配置格式；公开可配置范围应以 command、HTTP 与 prompt 为准。失败与超时按事件硬编码，没有 `onFailure`、`on_failure`、`failClosed` 一类把失败改成阻断的配置键（2026-11-04 复核，文档固定在提交 `35aa4b76ae10a458632d23c9b0052af5a31b3885`，PR #16196，2026-11-03T15:54:00Z，98,620 字节，三个键名全页均未出现）：`PermissionDenied` 与 `InstructionsLoaded` 都明确写 Qwen 会等 Hook 跑完再继续，Hook 失败只记录、不改变结果（前者输出与退出码被忽略，后者失败不阻止加载）；`PostToolBatch` 的 Hook 失败或 15 秒内没跑完时批次带着原结果继续（“If the hooks fail, or do not finish within 15 seconds, the batch continues with its results unchanged.”）；`MessageDisplay` 的最终投递最多等 5 秒，超时只在 stderr 打一条警告；`StopFailure`、`SessionDelete` 属即发即忘（后者输出与失败都无法撤销删除），`PostCompact` 的输出被忽略、`decision`/`continue`/`additionalContext` 与退出码都不起作用。`timeout` 默认值按 Handler 分开：command 60 秒（取值 ≥1000 仍按毫秒读以兼容旧配置，`"30s"` 这类非正数值被忽略并回落到 60 秒默认）、http 600 秒、prompt 30 秒，SDK 注册的 function Hook 继续用毫秒；`async: true` 的 command Hook 默认 60 秒并占用 10 个并发异步槽之一，10 个槽占满时新的异步 Hook 被跳过。设置文件读不了或解析失败时保留上一份设置快照与正在运行的 Hook、不改动文件并报错，系统级设置文件出问题时其中已有的 Hook 保持原样而其他修改仍生效；事件名不认识时跳过并给出 `Invalid hook event name` 警告。文档在输入结构一节提醒：对安全敏感的 Hook，解码器失败会改变 fail-open 或 fail-closed 行为，管理员必须在上线前拿升级后的载荷对照已部署的 Hook 验证；官方 HTTP judgment adapter 示例本身对网络、超时与畸形响应一律 fail open，以免后端故障挡住合法工具调用。headless 下 `UserPromptSubmit` 的门禁时点在 2026-10-16 改过：提交 `52ae53b02a17d6214e9e4f81578f2ba2725539a6`（PR #14356 “fix(hooks): gate headless file and skill prompts before expansion”）给官方 Hooks 文档新增一段，逐字为 “In headless runs, an input that invokes a custom command or skill, including several stacked skills, is checked before the command expands. The hook runs once for that input, with `prompt` and `submitted_prompt` both set to the raw input, such as `/deploy staging`. A block stops the command before it runs: its `!{...}` shell commands do not run, a skill does not register its allowed tools or hooks, and no model request is made. When the input is allowed, its `additionalContext` is added once to the expanded prompt, and `UserPromptExpansion` still runs after the expansion. Hooks that the skill registers do not check the input that invoked it. Other slash commands, MCP prompts and saved workflows, and the interactive UI and ACP sessions, still run the hook on the expanded prompt.”，即 headless 运行里调用自定义命令或 Skill（含多个堆叠 Skill）的输入改在命令展开前受检、Hook 对该输入只跑一次且 `prompt` 与 `submitted_prompt` 都是原始输入，阻断因此能挡掉命令内嵌的 `!{...}` shell 命令、Skill 的 allowed tools 与 hooks 注册以及随后的模型请求；放行时 `additionalContext` 只加一次到展开后的提示词，`UserPromptExpansion` 仍在展开后运行，而 Skill 自己注册的 Hook 不检查触发它的那条输入。这条只覆盖自定义命令与 Skill：其他 Slash 命令、MCP prompt 与保存的 Workflow，以及交互式 UI 与 ACP 会话，仍对展开后的提示词运行该 Hook。发布状态：v0.25.3-preview.0（2026-10-16T18:28Z，标签提交 `d407c0bb3823118281fbfc178494f4525a2241b8`）的发布说明逐字列出 “fix(hooks): gate headless file and skill prompts before expansion by @qqqys in #14356”，稳定通道 v0.25.3（2026-10-19T06:06:37Z 发布，标签提交 `b28b0d948fa28b89750ac5f1525b95e1746f47ab`）的 Bug Fixes 逐字列出 “fix(hooks): gate headless file and skill prompts before expansion (#14356)”，因此这条已随稳定通道发布（此前记录的「稳定通道最新 Release 仍是 v0.25.2、尚未包含」随之过期）。Mod API 的边界：`experimental.mods` 默认关闭，开启后还要求受信任工作区与一个已启用的 Claude 或原生 Qwen 扩展，Agent Plugins v1 便携包继续忽略 hooks，安全模式、bare 模式与 `disableAllHooks` 都阻止加载 mod；Surface 上交互终端（Ink 与 OpenTUI）与 headless（`qwen -p`，text/JSON/stream-json 输出）可用，而 ACP、managed/SSH、sandbox 与 session-agent 入口在本版本不运行 mod（官方逐字 “Mods do not run in ACP, managed/SSH, sandbox or session-agent entry points in this version.”）；权限上 mod 不能把原生 deny 或 ask 提升为 allow、mod 写的 `rule`/`hook`/`ceiling` 不能创建宿主权限、`sensitive: true` 显式不支持且没有密钥存储或配置 UI；交互终端 mod 命令随 PR #14268（提交 `2eafeebc556075b24c5a922f2b565254195e4692`，2026-10-16T10:20:23Z 合入 main）接入并经 v0.25.3 稳定通道发布，官方文档 `docs/users/extension/mods.md` 在标签 v0.25.3（提交 `b28b0d948fa28b89750ac5f1525b95e1746f47ab`，24,532 字节）下可取到，作者 API 基于 Claude Code 2.1.296 声明、再入行为遵循 Claude Code 2.1.301。**`FileChanged` 的版本边界与 Surface 条件**（2026-11-08 复核）：该能力随稳定版 v0.27.0（2026-11-07T20:45:25Z 发布，标签提交 `03b0c2435be1890a21b117860eaf389076787275`）进入，发布说明 Complete Change List 的 Features 首条逐字为 “Added FileChanged hooks for local non-managed sessions, reporting add, change, and unlink events for watched files and directories. (#13969) by @samuelhsin”，Breaking Changes 一节写 “No known breaking changes.”；上一个稳定版 v0.26.9（2026-11-06T16:07:57Z，标签提交 `600aff0592bcfc21668ca4aba95a5b20c6491b86`）下 `docs/users/features/file-changed-hooks.md` 与 `packages/core/src/hooks/file-changed-watcher.ts` 两个路径都返回 **HTTP 404**，同一标签下的 `docs/users/features/hooks.md` 事件表仍是 22 行且 `FileChanged` 与 `watchPaths` 均为 0 次命中，因此 **v0.27.0 是首个包含它的 Release**。**Surface 条件**逐字为 “SSH execution environments, shell sandbox workers, and managed dispatchers do not start a local filesystem watcher. Normal hook enablement and trust rules still apply. Watching stops when its owning session shuts down.”，专节的 Purpose 也把适用范围限定为 “explicitly watched files in local, non-managed sessions”；跨机器边界另有一句 “Roots in another container or remote instance need a watcher in that filesystem owner; registering their paths on the host does not observe them.”，多工作区共享目录则要自己在业务 Handler 里去重（“Independent sessions can observe the same shared root, so deduplicate the work in the business handler.”）。**Handler 覆盖面记为未确认**：文档 prompt Handler 一节写 “Prompt hooks can be used with most hook events, including:” 后只列 `PreToolUse`、`PostToolUse`、`Stop`、`SubagentStop` 与 `UserPromptSubmit` 五个，既没把 `FileChanged` 列进去也没排除它，因此该事件能否用 prompt 与 HTTP Handler 没有一手口径。官方也没有给出观察路径数量上限、单会话 watcher 数量上限或写入稳定的判定间隔——`awaitWriteFinish` 的 200 毫秒稳定阈值与 50 毫秒轮询间隔只能在源码里读到，文档只写 “Events are reported after writes settle.”；`SessionStart` 侧的注册是同步等待的（“Initial registration waits for watcher readiness before `SessionStart` returns.”），因此观察清单很大会拖慢会话启动，但官方没有给这条等待设上限。 |
| 证据状态 | 源码确认 |
| 来源 | [Qwen Code current Hooks](https://github.com/QwenLM/qwen-code/blob/8a44b1b9f79341a0faca9814fb1b57f0f1b354a2/docs/users/features/hooks.md)、[Qwen Code `docs/users/features/hooks.md`（失败与超时语义复核，2026-11-04 前移到提交 `35aa4b76ae10a458632d23c9b0052af5a31b3885`，PR #16196 “fix(core): require explicit trust for settings-file project hooks”，2026-11-03T15:54:00Z，98,620 字节，此前坐标 `d13ff87407d673c547726b43928dedba0465ffa1`）：**全页仍无 `onFailure`、`on_failure`、`failClosed` 或任何同类把失败改成阻断的配置键**。退出码表三行逐字未变——`0` “Success. A JSON object in `stdout` controls behavior. Any other `stdout`, including bare JSON values such as `42`, is plain text: it is added to the model context on `SessionStart`, `UserPromptSubmit` and `UserPromptExpansion`, and kept as a system message on other events. Output that looks like a JSON object but does not parse is never added to the model context.”、`2` “**Blocking error**. Ignores `stdout`, passes `stderr` as error feedback to the model.”、`Other` “Non-blocking error. `stderr` only shown in debug mode, execution continues.”；HTTP 侧逐字为 “HTTP hooks never follow redirects. A 3xx response is treated like any other non-2xx status: a non-blocking hook failure, and the redirect target is never contacted.”；官方 HTTP judgment adapter 示例逐字为 “Fails open on any network/timeout/malformed-response issue from the judgment backend, so an outage never blocks legitimate tool calls — same discipline the `command`-hook examples above apply with their own exit codes.”；`timeout` 单位与兼容规则逐字为 “`timeout` is in seconds for command, HTTP and prompt hooks; SDK-registered function hooks keep milliseconds.”、“Command hook timeouts used to be written in milliseconds, so for command hooks a value of `1000` or more is still read as milliseconds and existing settings keep working.” 与 “A command hook `timeout` that is not a positive number, such as `"30s"`, is ignored and the 60 second default applies.”，并有 “With debug logging enabled (`QWEN_DEBUG_LOG_FILE=1`), each command hook with a millisecond or ignored `timeout` is named once per session in that session's debug log.”；按事件硬编码的失败口径逐字为 “If the hooks fail, or do not finish within 15 seconds, the batch continues with its results unchanged.”（`PostToolBatch`）、“The turn's end (and the `Stop` hook, when it fires) waits up to 5 seconds for the final delivery to finish.” 与 “Hitting this timeout prints a warning on stderr.”（`MessageDisplay`）；事件表仍列 22 个事件。安全提醒逐字未变：“For security-sensitive hooks, a decoder failure can change fail-open or fail-closed behavior, so administrators must validate the upgraded payload against the deployed hook before rollout.”。该提交同时把设置文件里的项目 Hook 改成需要显式文件夹信任（新增 `security.folderTrust.enabled`、`security.allowedHttpHookUrls`、`security.allowPrivateNetworkHooks`、`disableAllHooks` 与 `stopHookBlockingCap` 等键的说明），属信任门禁而非失败语义、本次未处理；同批 v0.26.6/v0.26.7 发布说明里的 Mods `prompt.submit` 扩展（#15905、#16180）属 mod 事件覆盖面、本次也未处理](https://github.com/QwenLM/qwen-code/blob/35aa4b76ae10a458632d23c9b0052af5a31b3885/docs/users/features/hooks.md)、[Qwen Code `docs/users/features/hooks.md`（提交 `1dc74d58384ca1fe8c29730970f1f7c60d1e5265`，PR #14129 “feat(core): skip re-injecting an unchanged UserPromptSubmit hook context block”，2026-10-25T14:50:26Z，96,533 字节，2026-10-28 复核执行顺序与并发）：并发口径逐字未变——“By default, hooks execute in parallel for better performance”、“Use `sequential: true` in hook definition to enforce order-dependent execution”、“Sequential hooks can modify input for subsequent hooks in the chain”、“When hooks run in parallel, they all receive the same input, the last replacement in configuration order wins, and an invalid replacement from any of them stops the call. Hooks that run sequentially each receive the previous replacement as `tool_input`.”、“When several calls from one turn are scheduled together, as in the interactive terminal UI, every call's hooks run before any of them runs.”、“A shell command that a hook could still rewrite is not run in parallel with other tool calls.”；`MessageDisplay` 一类中途 Hook 另有 “At most one mid-stream hook execution per message is in flight at a time; while one runs, newer debounced payloads _replace_ the queued one rather than piling up behind it.”；`async: true` 逐字为 “Only `command` type supports asynchronous execution. Setting `"async": true` runs the hook in the background without blocking the main flow.” 与 “Occupies one of 10 concurrent async hook slots until it finishes or reaches its `timeout` (60 seconds by default). While all 10 slots are in use, a new async hook is skipped”；`## Hook Configuration` 一节另给出一条此前未记录的来源顺序口径，逐字为 “Hooks in the system settings files (System and SystemDefaults) load with the source System and, like user hooks, regardless of folder trust. Within one event, sequential hooks from settings and extensions run in this order: Project, User, System, Extension.”；事件表仍列出 22 个事件（`PreToolUse`、`PostToolUse`、`PostToolUseFailure`、`PostToolBatch`、`UserPromptSubmit`、`UserPromptExpansion`、`SessionStart`、`SessionEnd`、`SessionDelete`、`MessageDisplay`、`Stop`、`StopFailure`、`SubagentStart`、`SubagentStop`、`PreCompact`、`PostCompact`、`Notification`、`PermissionRequest`、`PermissionDenied`、`TodoCreated`、`TodoCompleted`、`InstructionsLoaded`）；相对此前记录的提交 `52ae53b02a17d6214e9e4f81578f2ba2725539a6`（96,225 字节）的唯一差异是 `UserPromptSubmit` 的 `additionalContext` 去重句 “If the sanitized payload is byte-identical to a block a hook already delivered earlier in the same session, it is not appended again, so a deterministic standing instruction reaches the model once rather than once per turn; this is unconditional with no opt-out, and any change to the payload re-injects it.”，与并发无关](https://github.com/QwenLM/qwen-code/blob/1dc74d58384ca1fe8c29730970f1f7c60d1e5265/docs/users/features/hooks.md)、[Qwen Code 提交 `52ae53b02a17d6214e9e4f81578f2ba2725539a6`（PR #14356 “fix(hooks): gate headless file and skill prompts before expansion”，2026-10-16 合入 main）在 `docs/users/features/hooks.md` 新增的段落逐字为 “In headless runs, an input that invokes a custom command or skill, including several stacked skills, is checked before the command expands. The hook runs once for that input, with `prompt` and `submitted_prompt` both set to the raw input, such as `/deploy staging`. A block stops the command before it runs: its `!{...}` shell commands do not run, a skill does not register its allowed tools or hooks, and no model request is made. When the input is allowed, its `additionalContext` is added once to the expanded prompt, and `UserPromptExpansion` still runs after the expansion. Hooks that the skill registers do not check the input that invoked it. Other slash commands, MCP prompts and saved workflows, and the interactive UI and ACP sessions, still run the hook on the expanded prompt.”](https://github.com/QwenLM/qwen-code/commit/52ae53b02a17d6214e9e4f81578f2ba2725539a6)、[Qwen Code `docs/users/features/hooks.md`（标签 v0.27.0，提交 `03b0c2435be1890a21b117860eaf389076787275`，105,235 字节，2026-11-08 复核）：**事件表由 22 个增加到 23 个，新增的是 `FileChanged`**，逐字行为 “| `FileChanged` | After an explicitly watched file is added, modified, or deleted | Basename of the changed file |”；同一文档在此前固定的提交 `1dc74d58384ca1fe8c29730970f1f7c60d1e5265`（96,533 字节）与 `35aa4b76ae10a458632d23c9b0052af5a31b3885`（98,620 字节）下都是 22 个事件、`FileChanged` 与 `watchPaths` 均为 0 次命中。`### Matcher Patterns` 表新增行逐字为 “| File Events | `FileChanged` | ✅ Regex | Basename of the changed file |”，表后专段逐字为 “For `FileChanged`, registering static paths uses literal filenames in cwd; filtering events uses the basename and the rules below. See FileChanged hooks for dynamic files and recursive directories.”，即**注册用字面文件名、过滤用正则**这两件事分开。`#### FileChanged` 一节逐字为 “**Purpose**: Observes additions, modifications, and deletions of explicitly watched files in local, non-managed sessions. Changes may come from Qwen Code, shell commands, editors, or other processes. The common input fields identify the listening session, including when another session writes a shared directory.”、“**Matcher**: Filters against the file basename using the existing matcher rules. Literal cwd filenames separated by `|` also register static paths. Omitted or empty matchers, `*`, and `.*` register no static paths; they receive events from the session's watched paths, including dynamically registered paths.”，事件字段为 `{ "file_path": "/home/me/work/demo/.envrc", "event": "change" }` 加 “`file_path` is absolute. `event` is `add`, `change`, or `unlink`. The initial directory scan is not replayed, but native filesystem events from a recent write may arrive after registration. Symbolic links are not traversed; register the real directory root. Notifications are best effort after writes settle. Hook output cannot veto or undo a write or add model context.”；输出选项逐字为 “Successful synchronous `hookSpecificOutput.watchPaths`, with `hookEventName: "FileChanged"`, replaces the dynamic watch list with absolute files or recursive directories. The last valid, explicit list in hook execution order wins; `[]` clears it, while omitted or invalid lists leave it unchanged. Async output does not register paths. Static registrations remain active.”，节末另写 “Memory directories are watched only when explicitly registered; there is no dedicated auto-memory enable/disable event.”。`SessionStart` 的输出选项新增一行逐字为 “`hookSpecificOutput.watchPaths`: absolute files or recursive directories to watch for `FileChanged`, from synchronous output with `hookEventName: "SessionStart"`. See FileChanged path registration for replacement, clearing, and validation rules.”。prompt Handler 一节仍写 “Prompt hooks can be used with most hook events, including:” 后列 `PreToolUse`、`PostToolUse`、`Stop`、`SubagentStop`、`UserPromptSubmit` 五个，**没有把 `FileChanged` 列入也没有排除**，该事件能否用 prompt 与 HTTP Handler 记为未确认；退出码三档、`timeout` 秒/毫秒兼容规则、并发与顺序口径（`sequential: true`、`async: true` 占 10 个槽）与失败语义本次逐字复核未变](https://github.com/QwenLM/qwen-code/blob/03b0c2435be1890a21b117860eaf389076787275/docs/users/features/hooks.md)、[Qwen Code `docs/users/features/file-changed-hooks.md`（标签 v0.27.0，提交 `03b0c2435be1890a21b117860eaf389076787275`，7,621 字节，`FileChanged` 专页；**该文件在标签 v0.26.9（提交 `600aff0592bcfc21668ca4aba95a5b20c6491b86`）下返回 404**，因此 v0.27.0 是首个包含它的 Release）：节首逐字为 “Use `FileChanged` to observe files changed by Qwen Code, shell commands, editors, or other processes. It runs in local, non-managed sessions.”。**输入归属**逐字为 “The common `session_id` and `cwd` belong to the listening session; they do not identify the writer. Multiple sessions watching a shared directory can each receive its changes. File content is not included.”，另写 “Only file events are delivered. Directory-self `addDir` and `unlinkDir` events are not delivered. Integrations tracking a watched root's own lifecycle must check filesystem state (for example with `stat`) and reconcile independently; a child's `unlink` does not prove that the root was removed.”。**静态注册**逐字为 “Add a `FileChanged` hook with literal filenames separated by `|`:”（示例 `"matcher": ".envrc|.env"`，命令用 `$QWEN_PROJECT_DIR`）、“This registers `.envrc` and `.env` in the session's cwd. Static registration does not expand globs or scan directories recursively.”、“Regex patterns filter already watched paths; register their files or directories explicitly through `watchPaths`.” 与 “Static roots accept only bare literal filenames, including dotfiles. Regex/glob metacharacters and path separators do not create static roots. Existing directories and paths that cannot be inspected are excluded; missing files may be watched for creation. Static file discovery observes their parent directory while excluding unrelated entries before traversal. Use explicit `watchPaths` for recursive directories. Changing the session working directory relocates static roots before relocation finishes; explicit absolute dynamic roots stay unchanged.”。**动态注册**逐字为 “A successful synchronous `SessionStart` hook can register absolute files or directories through `hookSpecificOutput.watchPaths`. Directories are watched recursively, including children created later. Recursive discovery skips `.git` and `node_modules` subtrees. To observe a path inside them, register that file or directory explicitly. Special file types are ignored, and ordinary directories have no depth limit. Initial registration waits for watcher readiness before `SessionStart` returns.”，另有 “Recursive discovery ignores atomic-write temporary files named `<target>.<12 lowercase hex characters>.tmp`, including delayed renames and orphans. Explicitly registering such a file still observes it. Directories with this suffix remain traversable, and other `.tmp` files are observed normally.”。**替换语义表**四行逐字为 “A list of absolute files or directories | Replaces the previous dynamic list”、“`[]` | Clears the dynamic list”、“Omitted or invalid `watchPaths` | Keeps the previous dynamic list”、“Output from an async hook | Does not register or change paths”，并写 “When several hooks return lists, the last valid, explicitly supplied list in hook execution order wins. All entries must be absolute paths; one invalid entry makes the list invalid. Static filename registrations remain active after a dynamic replacement or clear.”。**多工作区与远程边界**逐字为 “For several workspaces in the same filesystem, register all their actual memory directories in one list. Route notifications using `file_path` and your own directory-to-workspace mapping, rather than the listening session's `cwd`. Independent sessions can observe the same shared root, so deduplicate the work in the business handler. Roots in another container or remote instance need a watcher in that filesystem owner; registering their paths on the host does not observe them.”。**观察限制**逐字为 “The initial directory scan is not replayed. Native filesystem events from a recent write may arrive after registration. Explicitly registered symbolic-link files and directory roots observe their targets, including links in ancestor paths. Notifications use the configured alias; registering multiple aliases to one target produces a notification for each alias. Refresh registration after retargeting a link. Symbolic links discovered inside a recursive root are not traversed. Events are reported after writes settle.”、“Watching is best effort: filesystem coalescing, inaccessible paths, process downtime, and path reconfiguration can leave gaps. Integrations that require a complete synchronized copy must handle initial scans, reconciliation, and retry.”、“Watcher or hook failures are logged and leave the originating file operation unchanged.” 与 “`FileChanged` cannot veto or undo a write or add model context. Only explicitly registered paths are watched: memory folders are not registered automatically, and there is no dedicated auto-memory enable/disable event. A hook that writes a watched file can trigger itself; avoid those writes or deduplicate the work.”。**Surface 条件**逐字为 “SSH execution environments, shell sandbox workers, and managed dispatchers do not start a local filesystem watcher. Normal hook enablement and trust rules still apply. Watching stops when its owning session shuts down.”。官方给的示例用途是把 `/memory` 显示的记忆目录注册进观察清单，并提醒 “Choose a log or synchronization destination outside the watched paths to avoid triggering the handler with its own writes.”](https://github.com/QwenLM/qwen-code/blob/03b0c2435be1890a21b117860eaf389076787275/docs/users/features/file-changed-hooks.md)、[Qwen Code `packages/core/src/hooks/file-changed-watcher.ts`（标签 v0.27.0，提交 `03b0c2435be1890a21b117860eaf389076787275`，9,585 字节；**同一文件在标签 v0.26.9 下返回 404**）：实现基于 `import { watch, type FSWatcher } from 'chokidar'`，`watch(watchRoots, {...})` 的选项逐字为 `ignoreInitial: true`（对应文档 “The initial directory scan is not replayed”）、`followSymlinks: false`（对应 “Symbolic links discovered inside a recursive root are not traversed”）与 **`awaitWriteFinish: { stabilityThreshold: 200, pollInterval: 50 }`**，即文档只写 “Events are reported after writes settle” 的那个「settle」在源码里是**写入后稳定 200 毫秒、每 50 毫秒轮询一次**，官方文档没有给出这两个数字。`ignored` 回调逐字包含 `if (stats && !stats.isFile() && !stats.isDirectory()) return true;`（对应 “Special file types are ignored”）、原子写临时文件判断 `/\.[0-9a-f]{12}\.tmp$/.test(path.basename(file)) && !observed.includes(file)`（对应 “atomic-write temporary files named `<target>.<12 lowercase hex characters>.tmp`”，且显式注册的同名文件仍被观察）、以及动态根内的 `.git`／`node_modules` 跳过 `!path.relative(root, file).split(path.sep).some((part) => part === '.git' || part === 'node_modules')`；**没有传 `depth` 选项**，对应文档 “ordinary directories have no depth limit”。静态目标另有一条只允许目录的判断 `if (staticTargets.has(file)) return stats?.isDirectory() ?? | 字段 | 记录 |
| --- | --- |
| 矩阵结论 | `config.toml` · command · 同一事件命中的多条规则并行、`command` 完全相同只运行一次，官方未给并发上限 · 退出码 2 阻断，其他非零、超时与崩溃按 fail-open 放行；`[[hooks]]` 只接受 `event`/`matcher`/`command`/`timeout` 四个字段、多写即配置文件加载失败，因此没有也写不进 `onFailure` 一类失败开关 · 失败（无法启动、超时或错误退出码）记日志并在会话中显示一次警告，之后只记日志 · 无文件变更观察事件：2.5.2 的 20 个事件里 `watch`/`FileChanged`/`监听` 全部 0 次命中，唯一按时间触发的是每 60 秒的 `SessionHeartbeat`，且 `[[hooks]]` 的四字段限制也写不进 `watchPaths` 一类输出键 |
| 入口与配置 | 没有独立 `/hooks` 命令；在 `~/.kimi-code/config.toml` 的 `[[hooks]]` 中配置。 |
| 文件与目录 | 独立 Hook 位于用户 `config.toml`；Plugin manifest 也可携带 Hook 配置。 |
| 具体行为 | 可监听提示、工具、权限、会话、压缩与 Subagent 等事件。脚本的响应由退出码与 stdout 两样东西决定：退出码 `0` 表示放行、`2` 表示阻断、其他数字默认放行。返回值表逐字为 `0`「正常结束，放行」→「继续执行，若标准输出（stdout）有内容可附加到上下文」，`2`「主动阻断」→「停止当前操作；错误输出（stderr，`console.error` 打印的内容）作为阻断原因」，其他非零值「脚本出错」→「默认放行（fail-open）」，超时或崩溃「脚本异常」→「默认放行（fail-open）」。也可以在 stdout 返回一段 JSON 阻断，官方示例是 `hookSpecificOutput` 里的 `permissionDecision: "deny"` 加 `permissionDecisionReason`。只有可阻断事件（`PreToolUse`、`Stop`、`UserPromptSubmit`）的返回值会影响主流程，其余事件属观察型事件、触发后即发即忘，不管脚本返回什么主流程都不会改变。**没有文件变更观察事件**（2026-11-08 复核标签 `@moonshot-ai/kimi-code@2.5.2`、提交 `1c142a007c10e881602690def96057c23a2baa12` 下的 `docs/zh/customization/hooks.md`，9,523 字节）：`## 事件一览` 表共 20 行——`UserPromptSubmit`、`UserPromptQueued`、`PreToolUse`、`Stop`、`TurnStarted`、`PostToolUse`、`PostToolUseFailure`、`PermissionRequest`、`PermissionResult`、`SessionStart`、`SessionEnd`、`SessionHeartbeat`、`SubagentStart`、`SubagentStop`、`TaskStarted`、`StopFailure`、`Interrupt`、`PreCompact`、`PostCompact` 与 `Notification`，全文对 `watch`、`FileChanged`、`监听` 与 `变更` 均为 **0 次命中**；`文件` 的 3 次命中没有一次指文件系统观察（两处说明 Hook 的执行位置——“钩子始终在运行 Kimi Code 的机器上执行，因此在远程会话中它们读到的是本机的文件和进程，而非目标环境的。” 与 “没有项目级 hook 文件，因此远程目标上检出的内容永远无法向你的会话注入 hook 定义。”，一处是配置面的 “`[[hooks]]` 只允许这四个字段，多写会导致配置文件加载失败。”），写成 `变化` 的 2 次都指 `Notification` 的 “后台任务状态变化时触发”。唯一按时间而不是按动作触发的是 `SessionHeartbeat`，逐字为 “会话存活期间每 60 秒触发一次，仅配置本事件时计时器才运行；payload 含 `uptime_ms`；`kimi web` 卸载会话期间暂停，恢复后 `uptime_ms` 重新计时”，与文件系统无关。配置面也不给注册观察路径留余地：`[[hooks]]` 的四字段限制意味着 `watchPaths` 一类输出键在这一家连写进配置都会让配置文件加载失败。 |
| 作用域与优先级 | 用户配置与已启用 Plugin；当前文档未列项目级独立 Hook 文件。 |
| 扩展构成 | 独立配置当前只有 command Handler。 |
| 加载与刷新 | 启动时读取配置；Plugin 改动通常需要 `/reload` 或新会话。 |
| 适用界面 | 以 Kimi Code CLI 为准；ACP、Web UI 和外部编辑器只在对应能力中单独列出。 |
| 权限与信任 | Hook command 在本机执行；阻断与 fail-open 语义取决于退出码。文档给出明确边界：「即使脚本报错或超时，CLI 也不会因此中断你的工作」，这种“出错就放行”的设计称为 fail-open，用于避免 hook 异常阻塞主流程；紧随的 warning 写「正因为 fail-open，Hooks 适合做提醒和轻量拦截，但不应作为唯一的安全防线。对真正高风险的操作，仍需依赖权限审批和人工确认。」 |
| 条件与边界 | “命令表没有 `/hooks`”不等于没有 Hook 能力；Kimi 的入口是 TOML 配置。`[[hooks]]` 只允许 `event`（必填）、`matcher`（可选正则，不填匹配全部）、`command`（必填）、`timeout`（可选）四个字段，多写会导致配置文件加载失败，因此没有 `onFailure` 一类把失败改成阻断的开关，fail-open 不可配置。`timeout` 是整数秒、范围 1–600、默认 30 秒；非 Windows 平台上 hook 进程运行在独立进程组中，超时后 CLI 先发送信号让脚本有机会善后、再强制终止；hook 命令的工作目录是当前会话的项目目录。同一事件匹配多条规则时所有命中的 hook 并行运行，`command` 完全相同的多条规则只运行一次（2026-11-04 复核，该句在提交 `5f3ab5fac81e050fc2fa10787dd560655f377d16`（2026-10-29T12:43:07Z，9,523 字节）与此前记录的提交 `366fafbf921bb121d3906aa6b685f1045be4e54d`（8,549 字节）下逐字相同）。**失败处置在 2026-11-04 复核的文档里补上了明确口径**，逐字为 “当 hook 执行失败时（无法启动、超时或以错误退出码结束），CLI 会记录日志，并在会话中显示一次警告；之后的失败只记录日志。阻断决策保持 fail-open：失败的 hook 永远不会阻断操作。”——该段在历史坐标 `494df61ce9858c70a9f37a996614534bc9b1cd12`（8,319 字节）下不存在，**据此确认「命令无法启动」被官方明确归入失败形态之一、并按 fail-open 处理**，此前记录的「文档没有单独描述命令无法启动的情形，启动失败属于哪种取值记为未确认」随之过期；同时新增的两点是失败只在会话里警告一次、之后静默进日志，以及官方直接写下「失败的 hook 永远不会阻断操作」这句无条件结论。这条一次警告按 Hook、按事件还是按会话计数，官方没有说明，记为未确认。这条并行规则没有数字上限，`[[hooks]]` 也不接受 `sequential` 一类强制顺序执行的字段。工作目录有一条服务器路径修正：main 分支提交 `c1459a61845056832b85a0a220ee11a2bc5132f8`（PR #4242 “fix(server): run agent hooks in the session's working directory”，2026-10-15T11:38:24Z 合入）把 `AgentExternalHooksService` 的事件总线 Hook（`fireAndForgetTrigger(event)`）与 `PreToolUse`／`UserPromptSubmit`／`Stop`／`PreCompact` 四类 Agent 级 Hook 改为显式传 `cwd: sessionContext.cwd`；此前这些 Agent 级 Hook 没有传 `cwd`，Hook 运行器回落到 `bootstrap.cwd`，因而在引擎或服务器进程的启动目录里执行，与已经在会话目录执行的会话级 Hook 不一致。测试把引擎启动目录设为 `/launch-dir` 并断言 Agent 级 Hook 拿到会话目录 `/tmp`，`fullCompaction.test.ts` 另断言 `PreCompact`／`PostCompact` 载荷里的 `cwd` 是会话目录。该提交带 patch 级 changeset（`.changeset/hooks-session-cwd.md`，对 `@moonshot-ai/kimi-code` 与 `@moonshot-ai/kimi-code-sdk`），已随 Release `@moonshot-ai/kimi-code@2.3.0`（2026-10-21T05:51:43Z 发布）发布：`apps/kimi-code/CHANGELOG.md`（固定在提交 `4bad441fef34a6733f5c065ddea6c1a9c1c28e50`）的 `## 2.3.0` Patch Changes 逐字列出 “[#4242](https://github.com/MoonshotAI/kimi-code/pull/4242) [`c1459a6`](https://github.com/MoonshotAI/kimi-code/commit/c1459a61845056832b85a0a220ee11a2bc5132f8) Thanks [@wbxl2000](https://github.com/wbxl2000)! - Run hooks such as `PreToolUse` and `Stop` in the session's project directory, as documented, instead of the directory the server was started from.”，此前记录的「核对日期最新 Release 是 2.2.0、尚未发布」随之过期；官方中文 Hooks 文档没有提到这条服务器路径曾有差异，该文档自 2026-09-03 的 `494df61ce985` 之后改过两次：提交 `366fafbf921bb121d3906aa6b685f1045be4e54d`（PR #4284，2026-10-21T11:27:25Z）改 `SessionEnd` 与 `SessionHeartbeat` 两行、加入 `kimi web` 空闲卸载语义（属事件触发语义、本次未处理，该 PR 随 2.4.0 发布），提交 `5f3ab5fac81e050fc2fa10787dd560655f377d16`（2026-10-29T12:43:07Z，随 PR #4062 “feat: remote environments for SSH and Docker execution targets (phase 1: creation-time binding)”）把文件从 8,549 字节增到 9,523 字节并加入上面那段失败处置口径；2.3.0、`@moonshot-ai/kimi-code@2.4.0`（2026-10-28T11:45:13Z 发布）与核对日期最新稳定版 `@moonshot-ai/kimi-code@2.4.1`（2026-10-30T09:50:16Z 发布）都没有任何关于同一事件并发数、顺序执行或失败开关的条目，最新的 `2.5.0-canary.20261104024245-8fc5e22`（2026-11-04T02:57:42Z）同批提交也只涉及 wire 版本、FetchURL 私网地址、UTF-8 BOM 与 MCP OAuth 取消隔离，不涉及 Hook 失败语义。 |
| 证据状态 | 官方确认 |
| 来源 | [Kimi Code 中文 Hooks 文档 `docs/zh/customization/hooks.md`（提交 `366fafbf921bb121d3906aa6b685f1045be4e54d`，PR #4284 “feat(kap-server): reclaim idle sessions”，2026-10-21T11:27:25Z，8,549 字节，2026-10-28 复核执行顺序与并发）：并行句逐字未变——“**同一事件匹配多条规则时**，所有命中的 hook 并行运行；`command` 完全相同的多条规则只运行一次。”，仍没有任何数字上限，`[[hooks]]` 仍只允许 `event`、`matcher`、`command`、`timeout` 四个字段、多写会让配置文件加载失败，因此没有 `sequential` 一类强制顺序执行的字段；该文件自 2026-09-03 的 `494df61ce985` 之后只在这个提交改过 `SessionEnd` 与 `SessionHeartbeat` 两行，加入 `kimi web` 的空闲卸载语义（逐字为 “`kimi web` 将空闲会话从内存卸载时不触发，之后归档或删除这个已卸载的会话也不触发；透明恢复不触发 `SessionStart`” 与 “`kimi web` 卸载会话期间暂停，恢复后 `uptime_ms` 重新计时”），属事件触发语义、本次未处理，该 PR 随 Release `@moonshot-ai/kimi-code@2.4.0`（2026-10-28T11:45:13Z）发布](https://github.com/MoonshotAI/kimi-code/blob/366fafbf921bb121d3906aa6b685f1045be4e54d/docs/zh/customization/hooks.md)、[Kimi Code 中文 Hooks 文档 `docs/zh/customization/hooks.md`（fail-open 与超时语义复核，2026-11-04 前移到提交 `5f3ab5fac81e050fc2fa10787dd560655f377d16`，2026-10-29T12:43:07Z，9,523 字节，此前坐标 `494df61ce9858c70a9f37a996614534bc9b1cd12` 下同一文件为 8,319 字节）：**新增一整段失败语义**，逐字为 “当 hook 执行失败时（无法启动、超时或以错误退出码结束），CLI 会记录日志，并在会话中显示一次警告；之后的失败只记录日志。阻断决策保持 fail-open：失败的 hook 永远不会阻断操作。”——该段在历史坐标 `494df61ce985` 下不存在，**据此确认「命令无法启动」被官方归入失败形态之一且仍按 fail-open 处理**，此前记录的「文档没有单独描述命令无法启动的情形、启动失败属于哪种取值记为未确认」随之过期。退出码表四行逐字为 `0`「正常结束，放行」→「继续执行，若标准输出（stdout）有内容可附加到上下文」、`2`「主动阻断」→「停止当前操作；错误输出（stderr，`console.error` 打印的内容）作为阻断原因」、`其他非零值`「脚本出错」→「默认放行（fail-open）」、`超时或崩溃`「脚本异常」→「默认放行（fail-open）」；命名与边界两句逐字为 “即使脚本报错或超时，CLI 也**不会因此中断你的工作**。这种"出错就放行"的设计称为 fail-open（失败开放），避免 hook 异常阻塞主流程。” 与 “正因为 fail-open，Hooks 适合做提醒和轻量拦截，但**不应作为唯一的安全防线**。对真正高风险的操作，仍需依赖权限审批和人工确认。”；`timeout` 字段行逐字为 “`timeout` | `integer` | 否 | 超时秒数，范围 1–600；默认 30 秒”，超时处置逐字为 “非 Windows 平台上，hook 进程运行在独立进程组中；超时后 CLI 先发送信号让脚本有机会善后，再强制终止。”；可阻断与观察型事件的分界逐字为 “只有**可阻断事件**（`PreToolUse`、`Stop`、`UserPromptSubmit`）的返回值会影响主流程。其余事件属于**观察型事件**：触发后即发即忘，不管脚本返回什么，主流程都不会改变。”；配置面逐字为 “`[[hooks]]` 只允许这四个字段，多写会导致配置文件加载失败。”，四字段即 `event`、`matcher`、`command`、`timeout`，**因此写进 `onFailure` 一类键会直接让配置文件加载失败**；全页 `onFailure`、`sequential` ### Qoder CLI

| 字段 | 记录 |
| --- | --- |
| 矩阵结论 | `/hooks` · `settings.json` · command/HTTP/prompt/agent · 组级或条目级 `async` 与 `asyncRewake` 后台运行（后台 Hook 退出码 2 不阻断、改为唤醒模型）· 退出码 2 阻断，其他非零放行；无 `onFailure` 一类失败开关；官方没有公布同事件的执行顺序与并发数，1.1.78 起只在 Release Notes 提到显示 Hook 批次进度与等待时间 · `FileChanged` 的观察路径由 `CwdChanged` 或 `FileChanged` 输出的 `watchPaths`（“Absolute paths to register with the `FileChanged` watcher”）注册绝对路径，matcher 匹配 basename、输入为 `file_path` 与 `event`（`change`/`add`/`unlink`），退出码 2 不阻断；`SessionStart` 不能注册，watcher 实现、递归范围与初始观察清单来源均未确认 |
| 入口与配置 | `/hooks` 管理 Hook：官方 Slash 命令参考列出该行、描述逐字为 “Manage Hooks.”，没有给出 synopsis 或子命令，Release Notes 记 CLI 1.0.8（2026-05-28）“Made /hooks command generally available for all users”，而 Hooks 页本身没有描述这个命令。配置仍写在 User、Project 或 Local settings 的 `hooks` 字段与插件的 `hooks/hooks.json` 里。 |
| 文件与目录 | `~/.qoder/settings.json`、项目 `.qoder/settings.json` 与 `.qoder/settings.local.json`；Plugin 可携带 `hooks/hooks.json`。 |
| 具体行为 | 覆盖工具、提示、权限、通知、会话、压缩与 Subagent 等节点，并按 Handler 返回结果控制流程。官方事件表列出 23 个事件：`SessionStart`、`SessionEnd`、`UserPromptSubmit`、`PreToolUse`、`PostToolUse`、`PostToolUseFailure`、`PermissionRequest`、`PermissionDenied`、`Stop`、`StopFailure`、`SubagentStart`、`SubagentStop`、`PreCompact`、`PostCompact`、`Notification`、`InstructionsLoaded`、`ConfigChange`、`CwdChanged`、`FileChanged`、`WorktreeCreate`、`WorktreeRemove`、`Elicitation`、`ElicitationResult`。退出码语义逐字为：`0` 成功、stdout 按下述规则解析；`2` 阻断、stderr 内容反馈给 Agent（只对支持阻断的事件生效）；其他取值是非阻断错误，stdout 被忽略、stderr 写进诊断日志、主流程继续。JSON 里的 `decision` 取 `"allow"` 或 `"deny"`，`"deny"` 等价于退出码 2。支持阻断的事件及效果分别是：`UserPromptSubmit` 退出 2 拒绝该提示词并把 stderr 展示给用户，`PreToolUse` 退出 2 把 stderr 作为错误返回给 Agent，`Stop` 退出 2 把 stderr 作为消息注入对话让 Agent 继续工作，`SubagentStop` 退出 2 把 stderr 注入 Subagent 对话，`PreCompact` 退出 2 阻止本次压缩，`ConfigChange` 退出 2 阻止改动应用到当前会话，`Elicitation` 退出 2 拒绝该 elicitation，`ElicitationResult` 退出 2 把 action 改写为 `decline`。其余事件上退出 2 不阻断。**文件变更观察**（2026-11-08 复核 Hooks 页，HTML 1,268,339 字节、去标签后正文 32,243 字符）：`FileChanged` 与 `CwdChanged` 同属官方 “Working Directory and Files” 分组，此前本仓库只把 `FileChanged` 当事件名记进 23 个事件的清单里。事件总览表两行逐字为 “\| CwdChanged \| — \| — \| old_cwd, new_cwd \|” 与 “\| FileChanged \| filename basename \| — \| file_path, event \|”，即 `FileChanged` 的 matcher 匹配文件名 basename、**退出码 2 不阻断**（“exit 2 blocks” 一列为 “—”）。`FileChanged` 一节逐字为 “Triggered when a watched file changes.”、“**matcher field:** Basename of the changed file (supports exact match, `\|` multi-value, regex)”、附加输入字段 `{ "file_path": "/abs/path/file.ts", "event": "change" }` 加 “`event` values: `change` / `add` / `unlink`.”，输出为 “**hookSpecificOutput:** `additionalContext`, `watchPaths` (same as CwdChanged)”。观察路径的注册键定义只出现在 `CwdChanged` 一节的字段表里，逐字为 “`watchPaths` \| Absolute paths to register with the `FileChanged` watcher”，同节另有 `additionalContext` 的 “Context injected into the conversation” 与输入字段 `{ "old_cwd": "/old", "new_cwd": "/new" }`；`CwdChanged` 自己也不能阻断（总览表 matcher 与 exit 2 两列都是 “—”）。 |
| 作用域与优先级 | User、Project、Local 和 Plugin。 |
| 扩展构成 | command、HTTP、prompt 与 agent Handler。 |
| 加载与刷新 | 随设置和 Plugin 加载；修改后的刷新方式取决于对应配置或插件重载入口。 |
| 适用界面 | 以 Qoder CLI 为准；Agent SDK、ACP 和 Qoder IDE 中不同的入口会单独注明。 |
| 权限与信任 | 项目 Hook 只应在可信工作区启用；Hook 能阻断关键操作，但自身仍是本机可执行配置。`ConfigChange` 是唯一记录了强制生效例外的事件：`source` 为 `policy_settings` 时 Hook 仍会为审计目的触发，但改动强制执行、不能阻断。 |
| 条件与边界 | 不同 Handler 的超时、响应字段和阻断条件不同，需要按事件文档逐项设置。`timeout` 按秒配置且默认值随 Handler 变化：command 600 秒、http 600 秒、prompt 30 秒、agent 60 秒。没有 `onFailure`、`on_failure`、`failClosed` 一类把失败改成阻断的配置键（2026-11-04 复核，规范地址已改为 `https://docs.qoder.com/cli/hooks`，三个键名全页均未出现，阻断只能靠退出码 2），Hooks 页也没有描述 Hook 命令启动不了（例如可执行文件缺失）或超过 `timeout` 之后的具体后果，这两点记为未确认。按事件另有硬编码例外：`WorktreeCreate` 任何非零退出码都算失败，`StopFailure` 与 `InstructionsLoaded` 只是通知、输出与退出码被忽略，`WorktreeRemove` 也只是通知、失败通过 stderr 呈现。`async: true` 让同组 Hook 全部在后台运行、不阻断当前操作，结果作为额外上下文注入下一个模型回合；`asyncRewake: true` 在后台运行且退出码为 2 时由 CLI 用 stderr/stdout/error 拼一条 system reminder 唤醒模型（官方说明为 “useful for long-running checks”），配套字段 `rewakeMessage` 覆盖注入的 system 消息前缀、`rewakeSummary` 覆盖一行摘要（最多 300 字符）；`continue: false` 请求停止后续执行。Release Notes 另有几条只在发行说明出现、Hooks 页没有展开的失败与超时相关记录：CLI 1.0.29（2026-06-25）“Aligned SessionEnd hook timeout and PreToolUse fail-closed behavior with strict semantics” 与 “Tightened hook validation: hookEventName is now required in hookSpecificOutput”，其中 `PreToolUse` 的 fail-closed 具体覆盖哪些失败形态、与 Hooks 页“其他退出码为非阻断错误”的关系，页面没有说明，记为未确认；CLI 1.0.14（2026-06-04）“Fixed session freeze when a hook subprocess hangs on timeout or abort”；CLI 0.2.5（2026-04-29）“Fixed hook process hangs with exit event and tree-kill” 与 “Fixed hook permission decisions discarded on partial failure”；CLI 1.0.17（2026-06-10）“Enhanced asyncRewake hooks with custom messages, non-interactive downgrade, and prefix matching” 与 “Allowed plugin hooks to bypass the --setting-sources filter”；CLI 1.0.47（2026-07-16）“Fixed an issue where the PreToolUse hook did not take effect in headless and SDK modes”，说明该事件在 Print Mode 与 SDK 下也应生效；CLI 0.1.32（2026-03-18）“Updated Windows hook execution to run via Git Bash”，是 Hooks 页没有记录的平台条件。执行顺序与并发在 2026-10-28 复核后仍没有官方口径：Hooks 页（HTML 1,601,368 字节、去标签后正文 33,169 字符）没有任何说明同一事件的多个 Hook 并行还是顺序执行的语句，也没有并发数字，全页连 “batch” 一词都没有出现，与顺序相关的只有配置合并口径 “All three sources are **loaded and merged together** (hooks for the same event do not override each other)”；组级 `async`（“all hooks in the group run in the background without blocking the current operation”）、条目级 `async`（“this single hook runs in the background; overrides the group-level `async`”）与 `asyncRewake` 都没有并发上限，因此同事件的执行顺序与并发数记为未确认。公开 Release Notes 在 2026-11-04 复核时（HTML 1,419,034 字节）最新条目为 **CLI 1.1.82（November 3, 2026，“Session status reporting via OSC 7501 and reconnection fixes”）**，其前是 1.1.81（October 29, 2026，“Introducing CodeMode and More Reliable Session Continuity”），这两版都没有任何提到 Hook、退出码、超时、信任或阻断的条目；仍是最新 Hook 条目的三版为 CLI 1.1.80（October 27, 2026，四条里没有 Hook 条目）、1.1.79（October 26, 2026）的 “Fixed Hook progress from background Agent incorrectly showing in the TUI” 与 1.1.78（October 23, 2026，标题 “Hook Reliability Improvements and Safer File Encoding Handling”）的 “Fixed SessionStart Hook context being lost after compaction” 与 “Added display of Hook batch progress and waiting time” 两条，1.1.74（October 19, 2026）到 1.1.77（October 22, 2026）四版没有任何 Hook 条目；“Hook batch progress and waiting time” 只在 Release Notes 出现，Hooks 页没有 batch 的定义、也没有说明一个 batch 内的 Hook 是并行还是顺序执行，因此这条不能当作并发口径。事件表仍是 23 个，四类 Handler 的默认超时仍是 command 600 秒、http 600 秒、prompt 30 秒、agent 60 秒。**`FileChanged` 的未确认项**（2026-11-08 复核）：全页 `watchPaths` 只出现 2 次、`watcher` 与 `watched` 各 1 次，全部集中在 “Working Directory and Files” 那两节；`SessionStart` 的 `hookSpecificOutput` 逐字只有 “`additionalContext` (context injected into the conversation)” 加一句 “When the hook returns plain text (not JSON), the stdout is also injected into the conversation as context.”，`UserPromptSubmit` 的是 `additionalContext` 与 `sessionTitle`，因此 Qoder 只有 `CwdChanged` 与 `FileChanged` 两个事件能注册观察路径。由此产生的缺口是官方没有说明冷启动时第一个被观察的文件从哪里点名——`SessionStart` 不能注册，而 `FileChanged` 要先有被观察的文件才会触发；`FileChanged` 的 matcher 是否像 Claude Code 那样同时充当静态注册清单、还是纯过滤器，页面只写了 “supports exact match, `\|` multi-value, regex” 而没有给出注册语义。同样记为未确认的还有 watcher 的实现与依赖、目录是否递归、递归时是否跳过 `.git`／`node_modules`、写入稳定判定的间隔、符号链接处理、原子写临时文件处理、观察路径数量或目录深度上限，以及哪些 Surface 不启动 watcher；页面也没有自触发风险的提醒。发行版坐标同样定不下来：Release Notes 页 `https://changelog.qoder.com/cli`（2026-11-08 复核，HTML 782,358 字节、去标签后正文 69,863 字符）最新条目为 CLI 1.1.84（November 6, 2026，“Improves Session Compaction and MCP Status Display”），其前是 CLI 1.1.83（November 4, 2026，“Session Reliability Improvements and Enterprise Mode Controls”）与 CLI 1.1.82（November 3, 2026），全文对 `FileChanged`、`watchPaths`、`watcher` 与 `CwdChanged` 均为 **0 次命中**，因此这个事件从哪一版开始可用没有一手记录。 |
| 证据状态 | 未确认 |
| 来源 | [Qoder CLI Hooks](https://docs.qoder.com/en/cli/hooks)、[Qoder CLI Hooks 页（失败与超时语义复核，2026-11-04，规范地址已改为 `https://docs.qoder.com/cli/hooks`，`en/cli/hooks` 会跳过去）：**全页没有 `onFailure`、`on_failure`、`failClosed` 或任何把失败改成阻断的配置键，阻断只能靠退出码 2**。退出码三档逐字为 “Hooks control behavior through exit codes and stdout.”、“`0`: success; stdout is parsed according to the rules below.”、“`2`: blocking; stderr content is fed back to the Agent (only effective for events that support blocking).” 与 “Other values: non-blocking error; stdout is ignored, stderr is written to diagnostic logs, the main flow continues.”，JSON 侧另有 “`decision` … `allow` or `deny`, event-specific decision; `deny` is equivalent to exit 2.”；四类 Handler 的 `timeout` 逐字为 command “Timeout in seconds, default 600”、http “Timeout in seconds, default 600”、prompt “Timeout in seconds, default 30”、agent “Timeout in seconds, default 60”。页面**仍然没有描述 Hook 命令启动不了（例如可执行文件缺失）或超过 `timeout` 之后的具体后果**，这两点继续记为未确认。按事件硬编码的例外逐字为 `WorktreeCreate` “The hook must return the absolute path of the worktree; any non-zero exit code is treated as failure.”、`ConfigChange` “Blocking: exit 2 prevents the change from being applied to the current session. Exception: when source is policy_settings, hooks still fire for audit purposes but the change is enforced and cannot be blocked.”、`StopFailure` 与 `InstructionsLoaded` 各写 “Notification only — output and exit code are ignored.”、`WorktreeRemove` “Notification only — failures are surfaced via stderr.”；可阻断事件的效果分别是 `UserPromptSubmit` “Blocking: exit 2 rejects the prompt; stderr is shown to the user.”、`PreToolUse` “Blocking: exit 2; stderr is returned to the Agent as an error.”、`Stop` “Blocking: exit 2; stderr is injected into the conversation as a message and the Agent continues working.”、`SubagentStop` “Blocking: exit 2; stderr is injected into the sub-agent's conversation.”、`PreCompact` “Blocking: exit 2 prevents this compaction.”、`Elicitation` “Blocking: exit 2 declines the elicitation.” 与 `ElicitationResult` “Blocking: exit 2 rewrites action to decline.”；后台执行的 `asyncRewake` 逐字为 “When `true`, runs in the background; if it exits with code 2, the CLI builds a system reminder from stderr/stdout/error and wakes the model — useful for long-running checks”，**即后台 Hook 的退出码 2 不阻断任何动作、改成唤醒模型**](https://docs.qoder.com/cli/hooks)、[Qoder CLI Hooks 页的 “Working Directory and Files” 分组（2026-11-08 复核 `https://docs.qoder.com/cli/hooks`，HTML 1,268,339 字节、去标签后正文 32,243 字符）：`FileChanged` 与 `CwdChanged` 此前只作为事件名出现在本仓库记录的 23 个事件清单里，本次补录其字段与注册方式。事件总览表对应两行逐字为 “| CwdChanged | — | — | old_cwd, new_cwd |” 与 “| FileChanged | filename basename | — | file_path, event |”，即 `FileChanged` 的 matcher 匹配文件名 basename、**退出码 2 不阻断**。`FileChanged` 一节逐字为 “Triggered when a watched file changes.”、“**matcher field:** Basename of the changed file (supports exact match, `|` multi-value, regex)”、附加输入字段 `{ "file_path": "/abs/path/file.ts", "event": "change" }` 加 “`event` values: `change` / `add` / `unlink`.”、输出为 “**hookSpecificOutput:** `additionalContext`, `watchPaths` (same as CwdChanged)”。`watchPaths` 的定义在 `CwdChanged` 一节，逐字为 “Absolute paths to register with the `FileChanged` watcher”，同节给出 `{ "old_cwd": "/old", "new_cwd": "/new" }` 与 `additionalContext` 的 “Context injected into the conversation”。**全页 `watchPaths` 只出现 2 次、`watcher` 与 `watched` 各 1 次，都在上面这两节里**：`SessionStart` 的 `hookSpecificOutput` 逐字只有 “`additionalContext` (context injected into the conversation)” 加一句 “When the hook returns plain text (not JSON), the stdout is also injected into the conversation as context.”，**没有 `watchPaths`**，`UserPromptSubmit` 的是 `additionalContext` 与 `sessionTitle`，因此 Qoder 只有 `CwdChanged` 与 `FileChanged` 两个事件能注册观察路径。页面没有说明 watcher 的实现、递归与否、静态文件名注册、初始观察清单从哪来（既然 `SessionStart` 不能注册、`FileChanged` 又要先有被观察文件才会触发）、写入稳定判定、符号链接处理或哪些 Surface 不启动 watcher，这几点全部记为未确认；也没有给出观察路径数量或目录深度上限](https://docs.qoder.com/cli/hooks)、[Qoder CLI Hooks 页（2026-10-28 复核执行顺序与并发，HTML 1,601,368 字节、去标签后正文 33,169 字符）：全页仍没有任何说明匹配同一事件的多个 Hook 是并行还是顺序执行的语句，也没有数字形式的并发上限，全页连 “batch” 一词都没有出现，与执行顺序相关的只有配置合并口径 “All three sources are **loaded and merged together** (hooks for the same event do not override each other)”；事件表仍是 23 个（`SessionStart`、`SessionEnd`、`UserPromptSubmit`、`PreToolUse`、`PostToolUse`、`PostToolUseFailure`、`PermissionRequest`、`PermissionDenied`、`Stop`、`StopFailure`、`SubagentStart`、`SubagentStop`、`PreCompact`、`PostCompact`、`Notification`、`InstructionsLoaded`、`ConfigChange`、`CwdChanged`、`FileChanged`、`WorktreeCreate`、`WorktreeRemove`、`Elicitation`、`ElicitationResult`）；`timeout` 默认值仍是 command 600、http 600、prompt 30、agent 60；后台执行仍只有组级 `async`（“When `true`, all hooks in the group run in the background without blocking the current operation; results are injected as additional context in the next model turn”）、条目级 `async`（“When `true`, this single hook runs in the background; overrides the group-level `async`”）与 `asyncRewake`（“When `true`, runs in the background; if it exits with code 2, the CLI builds a system reminder from stderr/stdout/error and wakes the model — useful for long-running checks”），三者都没有并发数量限制](https://docs.qoder.com/en/cli/hooks)、[Qoder CLI Release Notes（2026-11-04 复核 Hook 相关条目，HTML 1,419,034 字节）：最新条目为 **CLI 1.1.82（November 3, 2026，“Session status reporting via OSC 7501 and reconnection fixes”）**，其前的 1.1.81（October 29, 2026，“Introducing CodeMode and More Reliable Session Continuity”）；这两版都没有任何提到 Hook、退出码、超时、信任或阻断的条目，因此 Hooks 页没有 `onFailure` 一类失败开关这条结论在发行说明侧也没有反证。仍是最新 Hook 条目的三版为 1.1.80（October 27, 2026，“Performance improvements and Auto Mode 2026）、1.1.75（October 20, 2026）与 1.1.74（October 19, 2026）四版没有任何 Hook 条目。“Hook batch progress and waiting time” 只在 Release Notes 出现，Hooks 页没有 batch 的定义、也没有说明一个 batch 内的 Hook 是并行还是顺序执行，因此同事件的执行顺序与并发数仍记为未确认](https://docs.qoder.com/release-notes/qoder-cli)、[Qoder CLI 官方更新日志（2026-11-08 复核 `https://changelog.qoder.com/cli`，HTML 782,358 字节、去标签后正文 69,863 字符；`https://docs.qoder.com/release-notes/qoder-cli` 仍以 307 跳转到该址）：全文对 `FileChanged`、`watchPaths`、`watcher` 与 `CwdChanged` 均为 **0 次命中**，因此无法给 `FileChanged` 事件定出发行版坐标。最新三条分别是 **CLI 1.1.84（November 6, 2026，“Improves Session Compaction and MCP Status Display”）**、**CLI 1.1.83（November 4, 2026，“Session Reliability Improvements and Enterprise Mode Controls”）** 与 CLI 1.1.82（November 3, 2026）；1.1.83 的条目逐字为 “The built-in `/verify` skill now requires explicit user invocation and can no longer be triggered automatically by the model”、“Fixed an error when resuming very large sessions with `/resume`”、“Added support for enterprise admins to disable Auto and YOLO modes”、“Fixed Windows installation corrupting Chinese directory names in the user PATH or adding duplicate Qoder CLI paths when using a non-UTF-8 code page”、“Improved retry logic so that after a successful retry of an oversized request, a subsequent oversized request can be retried again”、“Improved change detection for read files to prevent compaction from being triggered directly when content reaches the limit without any conversation” 与 “Fixed an issue where retrying in the same session could not resume writing conversation history after a file-busy co…”（页面文本在此截断），其中 “change detection for read files” 指读取内容的变更检测、与 Hook 的文件观察无关；1.1.84 的两条是 “Improved retention of recent responses and tool results so they are no longer lost after restarting and resuming a session” 与 “Fixed services pending authentication not correctly displaying their status in the `/mcp` command interface list”。仍是最新 Hook 条目的三版依旧为
