- [Claude Code Hooks](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/hooks)
- [Claude Code Hooks guide](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/hooks-guide)
- [Claude Code Hooks 参考页的 “Block the action when a hook fails” 一节与两张 Handler 字段表的 `onFailure` 行（2026-11-04 以 `.md` 形式复核，286,102 字节，比 2026-10-28 记录的 282,379 字节增加）：**官方参考页已完整文档化 `onFailure`**，此前记录的「官方 Hooks 参考的公共字段表与 Hooks 指南都没有出现 `onFailure`，取值集合、默认值与能否按事件或作用域覆盖记为未确认」随之过期。`#### Common fields` 表仍只有 `type`、`if`、`timeout`、`statusMessage` 与 `once` 五行，`onFailure` 不在公共字段里；`#### Command hook fields` 表逐字为 “`onFailure` | no | Whether the action that triggered the hook goes ahead when the hook fails: `"continue"` or `"block"`. See Block the action when a hook fails for `"block"` and for leaving the field unset. Requires Claude Code v2.1.295 or later”，`#### HTTP hook fields` 表逐字为 “`onFailure` | no | What happens to the action when the hook fails: `"continue", the default, or `"block"`. See Block the action when a hook fails. Requires Claude Code v2.1.295 or later”，**即取值集合就是 `"continue"` 与 `"block"` 两个、默认值是 `"continue"`**；`prompt`、`agent` 与 `mcp_tool` 三张字段表都没有该行。整节开头逐字为 “On most events, when a hook fails or times out, Claude Code still carries out the action. A policy hook with a wrong path or a crashing script then lets everything through. To block the action instead, set `"onFailure": "block"` on a `command` or `http` hook. Requires Claude Code v2.1.295 or later. For Agent SDK callback hooks, see Block the action when a callback fails.”。失败形态给出五类逐字定义——**Can't start** “a command hook fails to start, for example because the script or executable doesn't exist”、**Exit code other than 0 or 2** “counts for a command hook even if it printed JSON that allows the action, such as `permissionDecision: "allow"`. To return a JSON decision, exit 0”、**HTTP error** “an HTTP hook's connection fails, or the response status isn't 2xx”、**Timeout** “the hook reaches its `timeout`”、**Invalid output** “the JSON output can't be parsed or fails schema validation. For an HTTP hook, a 2xx body that is neither empty nor a JSON object also counts. Plain-text stdout from a command hook isn't a failure”。效果口径逐字为 “With `"block"` set, a failure does what exit code 2 does on that event, except on `PermissionRequest`, where it denies the request. For example, a `PreToolUse` failure blocks the tool call and a `UserPromptSubmit` failure blocks the prompt.”，与同页 “Exit code 2 behavior per event” 表里 `PermissionRequest` 行的 “Exit code 2 isn't honored for this event and the permission flow proceeds unchanged. Deny through the `decision` object instead” 相反，**即 `onFailure: "block"` 在 `PermissionRequest` 上比退出码 2 更强**。无效范围逐字为 “The field has no effect on these hooks: **`Stop`, `SubagentStop`, `TaskCompleted`, and `TeammateIdle` hooks**: exit code 2 on these events sends Claude back to keep working, and Claude can't repair a hook that won't run；**Background command hooks**: command hooks that set `async` or `asyncRewake`”。用户可见文案为 `PreToolUse:Bash hook error: [node ${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR}/.claude/hooks/check-command.js]: failed; blocking because onFailure is "block"` 加解释器自己的错误输出，超时后把 `failed` 换成 `timed out`；官方给的验证办法是故意让 `check-command.js` 缺失再让 Claude 跑 `ls`，并写 “Without `onFailure` set, the same missing script is a non-blocking error and `ls` runs.”。`#### Timeouts` 一节逐字为 “On `PreToolUse`, a timed-out `command`, `http`, or `mcp_tool` hook doesn't block the tool call, except for hooks whose failure blocks the action.” 与 “To block the call when a `command` or `http` hook times out, set `onFailure: "block"`.”；`#### Other exit codes` 一节把三处 non-blocking 说明都加上 “Unless the hook is one whose failure blocks the action” 的前置条件，并在 WARNING 里写 “Exit code 1 without valid JSON output is a non-blocking error unless the hook is one whose failure blocks the action, even though 1 is the conventional Unix failure code. If your hook is meant to enforce a policy, use `exit 2`.”](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/hooks#block-the-action-when-a-hook-fails)
- [Claude Code Hooks 参考页的 `### FileChanged`、`### CwdChanged`、`### DirectoryAdded` 三节与 `#### SessionStart decision control` 表（2026-11-08 以 `.md` 形式复核，289,929 字节，比 2026-11-04 记录的 286,102 字节增加；此前各次复核都只取失败语义与并发段落，本节内容首次记录）：事件汇总表行逐字为 “`FileChanged` | When a watched file changes on disk. The `matcher` field specifies which filenames to watch”。整节开头逐字为 “Runs when a watched file changes on disk. Claude Code detects changes with a filesystem watcher, not by inspecting tool calls, so it runs the hook no matter what changed the file: an `Edit` or `Write` tool call, a script Claude runs with `Bash`, or a process outside Claude Code entirely. A common use is reloading environment variables when project configuration files change.”，即**观察的是文件系统而不是工具调用**。**`matcher` 在这一事件上承担两个角色**，逐字为 “**Build the watch list**: the value is split on `|` and each segment is registered as a literal filename in the working directory, so `".envrc|.env"` watches exactly those two files. Regex patterns are not useful here: a value like `^\.env` would watch a file literally named `^\.env`.” 与 “**Filter which hooks run**: when a watched file changes, the same value filters which hook groups run using the standard matcher rules against the changed file's basename.”；`### Matcher patterns` 的例外句逐字为 “`FileChanged` and `StopFailure` use a narrower exact-match set of letters, digits, `_`, and `|` only. A hyphen, space, or comma in a matcher for those two events keeps it on the regular-expression path, and only `|` separates alternatives.” 与 “The `FileChanged` event doesn't follow these rules when building its watch list.”，matcher 支持表对应行逐字为 “| `FileChanged` | literal filenames to watch (see FileChanged) | `.envrc\|.env` |”。**动态观察清单**逐字为 “To watch files you can't name up front, return `watchPaths` from a hook to update the watch list dynamically. Claude Code starts the watcher only when something names a file to watch, so seed the list with a FileChanged group whose matcher names at least one file, or with a SessionStart or CwdChanged hook that returns `watchPaths`. The matcher still filters which hook groups run when a watched file changes, so give the group that handles dynamic paths an omitted matcher, which matches every watched file and adds nothing to the watch list. A `"*"` matcher also matches every file, but Claude Code registers it in the watch list like any other value, as a literal file named `*`.”，即 **watcher 只在有东西点名文件时才启动**，三个入口是 `FileChanged` 组的字面 matcher、`SessionStart` 与 `CwdChanged` 的 `watchPaths`。输入字段表逐字为 “`file_path` | Absolute path to the file that changed” 与 “`event` | What happened: `"change"` for a modified file, `"add"` for a created file, or `"unlink"` for a deleted file”；输出字段表逐字为 “`watchPaths` | Array of absolute paths. Replaces the current dynamic watch list. Paths from your `matcher` configuration are always watched. Use this when your hook script discovers additional files to watch based on the changed file”。**该事件没有决策控制**，逐字为 “FileChanged hooks have no decision control. They can't block the file change from occurring.” 与 “Claude Code reads `watchPaths` and `systemMessage` from their JSON output and discards `continue`. In interactive sessions, it shows the `systemMessage` as a brief terminal notification. The message doesn't reach the SDK message stream.”；“Exit code 2 behavior per event” 表对应行逐字为 “| `FileChanged` | No | Shows stderr to user only |”，Decision control 表把它与 “Setup, Notification, SessionEnd, PostCompact, InstructionsLoaded, StopFailure, CwdChanged, DirectoryAdded” 并列为一行 “None | No decision control. Used for side effects like logging or cleanup”。Handler 类型限制逐字为 “Events that support `command`, `http`, and `mcp_tool` hooks but not `prompt` or `agent`:” 下列出 `FileChanged`（同列还有 `ConfigChange`、`CwdChanged`、`DirectoryAdded`、`InstructionsLoaded`、`MessageDisplay` 等）。环境变量逐字为 “FileChanged hooks have access to `CLAUDE_ENV_FILE`. Variables written to that file persist into subsequent Bash commands until the next CwdChanged event, when Claude Code clears them.” 与 “`CLAUDE_ENV_FILE` is available for SessionStart, Setup, CwdChanged, and FileChanged hooks. Other hook events don't have access to this variable, and neither does a hook that runs in PowerShell, whether through `"shell": "powershell"` or by default on Windows without Git Bash.”。**自触发风险**由官方示例的说明给出，逐字为 “Its `grep` guard tests for the same thing `perl` removes, a CR at the end of a line, so the run after a normalization exits without touching the file. A looser guard loops forever, because `perl -i` rewrites the file even when it substitutes nothing and Claude Code runs the hook again after every rewrite.”。`SessionStart` 侧的注册字段逐字为 “`watchPaths` | Array of absolute paths to watch for FileChanged events during this session”，同表另有 `additionalContext`、`initialUserMessage`、`sessionTitle` 与 `reloadSkills`；`CwdChanged` 侧逐字为 “`watchPaths` | Array of absolute paths. Replaces the current dynamic watch list. Paths from your `matcher` configuration are always watched. Returning an empty array clears the dynamic list, which is typical when entering a new directory”，该节另写 “CwdChanged doesn't support matchers and fires on every occurrence.”、“CwdChanged hooks have no decision control. They can't block the directory change.” 与 “Pairs with FileChanged for tools like direnv that manage per-directory environment.”，输入字段为 `old_cwd` 与 `new_cwd`。`DirectoryAdded` 一节逐字为 “Runs after you add a working directory mid-session with the `/add-dir` command, or after an SDK client adds one with the `register_repo_root` control request.”，不触发的三种情形是 “You pass a directory with the `--add-dir` startup flag; SessionStart covers those directories”、“You add a directory on the `/permissions` Workspace tab” 与 “You add a directory that is already a working directory or inside one”，另写 “Claude Code doesn't wait for the hook: the add completes immediately, and the hook runs in the background with the 600-second default timeout.” 与 “Claude Code fires DirectoryAdded after refreshing sandbox and permission state, so sandboxed tools already see the new directory when your hook runs. Hook commands themselves run unsandboxed.”，matcher 取 `slash_command` 或 `register_repo_root`，输入字段为 `directory` 与 `source`。官方两处把该事件定位为工具匹配的替代方案：`### PreToolUse` 一节写 “To run a hook when a specific file changes on disk, whatever wrote it, use FileChanged instead of matching file-editing tools by name. Unlike PreToolUse, Claude Code runs FileChanged hooks after the change, and they have no decision control, so they can't block the write.”，`### PostToolUse` 一节写 “To run a hook when a specific file changes on disk, whatever wrote it, use FileChanged. Claude Code doesn't run a `PostToolUse` hook matching `Edit|Write` when a `Bash` command or a process outside Claude Code rewrites the same file.”；生命周期图把 `FileChanged` 与 `CwdChanged`、`DirectoryAdded`、`ConfigChange`、`Notification` 一起归为 “standalone async events”](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/hooks#filechanged)
- [Claude Code v2.1.83 更新日志（`CHANGELOG.md` 固定到标签提交 `cada21c89dc76cb0355fdeee659806674abfd38c`）：`## 2.1.83` 段里逐字列出 “Added `CwdChanged` and `FileChanged` hook events for reactive environment management (e.g., direnv)”，即两个事件同版引入、官方给出的用途就是 direnv 一类按目录管理环境的工具。同段另有 “Added `managed-settings.d/` drop-in directory alongside `managed-settings.json`, letting separate teams deploy independent policy fragments that merge alphabetically”、“Added `sandbox.failIfUnavailable` setting to exit with an error when sandbox is enabled but cannot start, instead of running unsandboxed”、“Added `disableDeepLinkRegistration` setting to prevent `claude-cli://` protocol handler registration” 与 “Added `CLAUDE_CODE_SUBPROCESS_ENV_SCRUB=1` to strip Anthropic and cloud provider credentials from subprocess environments (Bash tool, hooks, MCP stdio servers)”，属其他能力字段、本次未处理。`DirectoryAdded` 晚得多，逐字条目 “Added `DirectoryAdded` hook that fires after `/add-dir` or the SDK `register_repo_root` control request registers a new working directory mid-session” 属 v2.1.219（固定到标签提交 `0c188278cdf9b8b717c2740550bdb0f69c02f6b5`）。2026-11-08 复核 main 的 `CHANGELOG.md`（1,176,779 字节、最新段 `## 2.1.319`）：全文里 `FileChanged`、`DirectoryAdded` 与 `watchPaths` 一共只有上述两条命中，`watchPaths` 这个词从未出现在更新日志里，v2.1.83 到 v2.1.319 之间没有任何改变观察清单语义、watcher 启动条件或 `FileChanged` 阻断能力的条目](https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code/blob/cada21c89dc76cb0355fdeee659806674abfd38c/CHANGELOG.md)
- [Claude Code Hooks 参考页的 “Failed hooks on Windows without Git Bash” 一节（2026-11-04 复核，286,102 字节）：这是一条**平台条件的 fail-closed 默认**，与 `onFailure` 互为镜像。逐字为 “If the error for a blocked tool call includes this text, a `PreToolUse` or `PermissionRequest` command hook failed or timed out and blocked the call:” 加错误文案 `blocking because Git Bash wasn't found, so only exit code 0 or 2 counts as this hook's verdict.`，随后 “Without Git Bash, any exit code other than 0 or 2 from that hook counts as a failure, even when it prints JSON that doesn't deny the call.”；节首的 NOTE 逐字为 “On Windows without Git Bash, a shell-form `PreToolUse` or `PermissionRequest` command hook that fails with `onFailure` unset can instead block the tool call.”，即**只覆盖 shell 形式（未设 `shell`）的 command Hook、只覆盖这两个事件、且只在 `onFailure` 未设时成立**。官方给出五条修法，其中恢复放行的一条是 “**The call should go ahead when the hook fails**: add `"onFailure": "continue"` to the hook”；“**The hook exits 1 to warn but must block when it crashes**: with `"onFailure": "continue"`, the call goes ahead on exit code 1, warning or crash alike. Add `"onFailure": "block"` instead, and have the script exit 0 where it warns, so those calls go ahead. To still show the warning on `PreToolUse`, print it to stdout as the `systemMessage` field of one JSON object.”；“**The hook is written for Bash**: without Git Bash, a shell-form hook with no `shell` set runs in PowerShell, where it can fail. Install Git for Windows, or set `CLAUDE_CODE_GIT_BASH_PATH` if Git Bash is installed where Claude Code doesn't find it. Then start a new session.”；“**The hook runs one `.exe`, not a `.cmd` or `.bat` file**: put the program in `command` and its arguments in `args` to use exec form, and add `"onFailure": "block"`. Claude Code then starts the program with no shell. Without `"onFailure": "block"`, the call can go ahead when the program fails, is missing, or times out”；“**A PowerShell guard hook that must block on failure**: with `onFailure` unset, it stops doing so once Git for Windows is installed and Claude Code finds Git Bash. Add `"onFailure": "block"` so a failure or a timeout blocks the call with or without Git Bash. Also set `"shell": "powershell"` so the hook keeps running in PowerShell from then on instead of in Git Bash”。文案版本条件逐字为 “Before v2.1.309, the message read `blocking because Git Bash wasn't found and this hook doesn't set "shell" to "powershell", so only exit code 0 or 2 counts as its verdict.`”；错误里另有 `PowerShell started no program` 时指向 “the fixes for a `command` that is only quoted strings”，与 v2.1.310 更新日志的 “Windows: Fixed PreToolUse and PermissionRequest hooks letting a tool call through when Git Bash is missing and the hook's command is only quoted strings, such as a quoted path” 是同一条线索](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/hooks#failed-hooks-on-windows-without-git-bash)
- [Claude Code Hooks 指南页（2026-11-04 以 `.md` 形式复核，66,413 字节，比 2026-10-28 记录的 66,090 字节增加）：`onFailure` 已出现在指南正文两处。`#### Hook output` 小节逐字为 “With an exit code besides 0 and 2 and no valid JSON output, the action proceeds on most events, unless the hook is one whose failure blocks the action. A hook with `onFailure` set to `"block"` is one example. Exit 0 to report no objection, or exit 2 to block the action:”；`## HTTP hooks` 小节逐字为 “**2xx status**: to block a tool call, return the appropriate `hookSpecificOutput` fields in the body.” 与 “**Any other status, or the request fails**: Claude Code reports a non-blocking error and lets the action continue. To make a failed endpoint block the action, set `onFailure: "block"` on the hook.”，两处都把细节链回参考页的 “Block the action when a hook fails” 锚点。此前记录的「Hooks 指南在核对日期没有出现 `onFailure`」随之过期](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/hooks-guide)
- [Claude Code Agent SDK Hooks 页的 “Block the action when a callback fails” 一节与 Matchers 表（2026-11-04 以 `.md` 形式复核，49,119 字节）：Matchers 表新增一行逐字为 “`onFailure` | `"continue" | "block"` | `undefined` | What a failed callback does. Set `"block"` to block the action when a callback fails. In Python, use `on_failure`”，**即该字段写在 matcher 上而不是单个回调上、默认值 `undefined`、Python 侧键名是 `on_failure`**。整节开头逐字为 “On most events, if a callback throws or returns output that fails validation, the action goes ahead and your app receives an `SDKInformationalMessage` warning whose `tag` is `hook_callback_failed`. A `PreToolUse` guard that crashes then lets the tool call reach the normal permission flow. A callback that returns nothing, such as a path with no `return`, also counts as failing. In Python, read `tag` from `SystemMessage.data`.”，随后 “To block the action instead, set `onFailure: "block"` in TypeScript or `on_failure="block"` in Python on the matcher. Requires TypeScript Agent SDK v0.3.317 or later, or Python Agent SDK v0.2.177 or later.”，官方示例为 Python 的 `HookMatcher(matcher="Bash", hooks=[check_policy], on_failure="block")` 与 TypeScript 的 `PreToolUse: [{ matcher: "Bash", hooks: [checkPolicy], onFailure: "block" }]`；阻断后的表现逐字为 “The tool result includes `failed; blocking because onFailure is "block"`, and Claude doesn't see your error text.”，并提醒 “Return `{}` when the callback has no opinion. With `"block"` set, a path with no `return` blocks the action. A callback that catches its own error and returns `{}` isn't failing, so let the error propagate from a guard.”。`#### Events that behave differently` 逐字列出三条——“**`Stop`, `SubagentStop`, `TaskCompleted`, and `TeammateIdle`**: these events ignore the option, so the action goes ahead.”、“**`PermissionRequest`**: the block counts only if it arrives before your `canUseTool` callback answers.” 与 “**`PostToolUse`**: the tool has already run, so Claude receives the failure as feedback.”；超时一句逐字为 “A callback that exceeds its timeout follows Hook timeout. With `"block"` set, a timeout also blocks `PreCompact`, `Elicitation`, and `WorktreeCreate`.”，而同页 “Hook timeout” 一节给出不设 `"block"` 时的逐事件默认——`PreToolUse` 阻断该次工具调用、`PostToolUse` 与 `PostToolUseFailure` 保留工具结果继续、`UserPromptSubmit` 与 `UserPromptExpansion` 阻断提示词并给出点名该 Hook 与超时的消息、`Stop` 与 `SubagentStop` 算没有决定、`SessionStart` 算没有输出、`PreModelSwitch` 阻断模型切换、其他事件（如 `Notification`、`PreCompact`、`PostModelSwitch`）只记录并继续。`#### Startup errors for onFailure` 逐字为 “With `"block"` set, the query fails at startup and sends no prompt when it can't honor the option:”，两条分别是 “**Claude Code doesn't support the option**: the error includes `does not honor`. Update Claude Code, or remove the option.” 与 “**The session was already running and didn't register your hooks**: the error includes `did not register your hooks`. Start a new session.”；参考页的 `onFailure` 一节也把 SDK 回调指向这一锚点](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/agent-sdk/hooks#block-the-action-when-a-callback-fails)
- [Claude Code v2.1.295 Hook `onFailure: "block"` 更新日志](https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code/blob/602df92bf481ed904533e95c09f740f40aab5aed/CHANGELOG.md)
- [Claude Code v2.1.317 更新日志（2026-11-03T01:25:46Z 发布，CHANGELOG.md 固定到提交 `964d69565952a176ac1975ed218362991db71433`，2026-11-03T01:25:28Z，1,142,787 字节，最新段为 `## 2.1.317`）：与 Hook 失败语义相关的一条逐字为 “Added `onFailure: "block"` for Agent SDK hook callbacks, so a callback that fails blocks the action instead of letting it run”，即把此前只覆盖 settings 侧 command 与 HTTP Hook 的 `onFailure` 扩到 Agent SDK 的回调 Hook；同版另有一条与阻断相关的安全检查 “Added a check that asks before a web or shell tool call sends your account email address to the network”，属权限字段、本次未处理。核对日期最新 Release 为 v2.1.318（2026-11-04T00:01:03Z），其更新日志没有 Hook 失败语义条目；v2.1.316 有一条 “Fixed PowerShell hooks not blocking on exit code 2 when the command begins with a type cast, `param`, `using` or an attribute”，v2.1.314 有三条 “Fixed a PreCompact hook with very large output making compaction fail or repeat; each hook's output is now cut at 10,000 characters”、“Fixed hooks matching Bash not running on shell commands started by the Monitor tool; such hooks can block or ask, but not approve or rewrite those commands” 与 “Changed hook `if` conditions such as `Monitor(tail *)` to match the Monitor tool's shell commands as `Bash(tail *)` matches Bash's”，都属退出码阻断与事件匹配、不改变 `onFailure` 的取值或默认值](https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code/blob/964d69565952a176ac1975ed218362991db71433/CHANGELOG.md)
- [Claude Code v2.1.302 更新日志（固定到提交 `c017428c8c27e604840435048d5bce6a9a22c040`）：与生命周期 Hooks 相关的一条逐字为 “Changed prompt and agent hooks so that at most 12 of one event run at a time; the rest wait their turn”，该段没有说明 command、http 与 `mcp_tool` 三类 Handler 是否同受这个上限、12 是按事件还是按会话计，也没有说明排队等待的时间是否计入 Hook 自身的 `timeout`；v2.1.312 起这条「同一事件至多 12 个」被「每会话至多 24 个、含 Subagent」取代](https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code/blob/c017428c8c27e604840435048d5bce6a9a22c040/CHANGELOG.md)
- [Claude Code v2.1.312 更新日志（固定到提交 `0e1f4789406477380b267a01f3ddd50b0211e5b3`，该提交于 2026-10-27T20:23:56Z 更新 `CHANGELOG.md` 与 `feed.xml`；对应 Release v2.1.312 于 2026-10-27T20:24:20Z 发布，是核对日期最新 Release）：与 Hooks 并发相关的一条逐字为 “Changed prompt and agent hooks to run at most 24 at a time per session, sub-agents included, plus at most 12 that have waited longer than their event's timeout; a `timeout` under 1 second now counts as 1 second”，即把 v2.1.302 的「同一事件至多 12 个」改为「每会话至多 24 个、Subagent 计入同一预算」，另外容许至多 12 个等待时间已超过其事件 `timeout` 的排队 Hook，并给 `timeout` 加 1 秒下限；该条仍没有说明 command、http 与 `mcp_tool` 三类 Handler 是否同受这两个数字约束，也没有说明 24 与 12 相加是否意味着同一会话可以有 36 个 prompt/agent Hook 同时在跑。同版另有两条 Hook 条目属其他方面、本次未处理：v2.1.311 的 “Fixed `SubagentStop` hooks running about twice a minute for each running subagent instead of once when it finishes” 与 v2.1.310 的 “Windows: Fixed PreToolUse and PermissionRequest hooks letting a tool call through when Git Bash is missing and the hook's command is only quoted strings, such as a quoted path”](https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code/blob/0e1f4789406477380b267a01f3ddd50b0211e5b3/CHANGELOG.md)
- [Claude Code Hooks 参考页的 “Hook handler fields” 一节（2026-10-28 抓取，282,379 字节，比 2026-10-19 记录的 273,668 字节增加；**2026-11-04 复核，该页已增至 286,102 字节，但并发段落逐字未变、全页仍没有出现 24 这个数字，也没有「每会话」或「含 Subagent」的措辞**）：并发段落逐字为 “When an event fires, its matching hooks run in parallel. If more than 12 of them are prompt or agent hooks, 12 of those run at once and the rest start as running ones finish. Each waiting hook's [`timeout`](#common-fields) starts counting when that hook starts.”，紧接一段逐字为 “If you define the same handler in more than one settings file, it runs once. A plugin's or skill's copy of the same handler stays separate.”；这是官方参考页第一次写出并发数字，此前记为未确认的两点因此有了口径——数字只约束 prompt 与 agent 两类 Handler（该句没有给 command、http 与 `mcp_tool` 任何上限），排队等待的时间不计入 Hook 自身的 `timeout`（`timeout` 从该 Hook 开始运行时才起算）；但该页仍写「同一事件 12 个」，没有出现 v2.1.312 更新日志的 24 与「每会话、含 Subagent」口径，全页也没有 `timeout` 低于 1 秒按 1 秒算的说明：`#### Common fields` 的 `timeout` 行逐字为 “Seconds before canceling. Claude Code doesn't enforce it on a command hook you run with [`async: true`](#run-hooks-in-the-background). Defaults: 600 for `command`, `http`, and `mcp_tool`; 30 for `prompt`; 60 for `agent`. Claude Code lowers the `command`, `http`, and `mcp_tool` default to 30 on [`UserPromptSubmit`](#userpromptsubmit), [`PreModelSwitch`](#premodelswitch), and [`PostModelSwitch`](#postmodelswitch), and to 10 on [`MessageDisplay`](#messagedisplay). [`SessionEnd`](#sessionend) hooks share a 1.5-second budget; if your settings set a longer per-hook `timeout`, Claude Code raises the budget to match, up to 60 seconds”（2026-11-04 复核该行逐字未变，公共字段表仍只有 `type`、`if`、`timeout`、`statusMessage`、`once` 五行），`#### Timeouts` 一节也没有下限说明；Hooks 指南页（2026-10-28 抓取 66,090 字节，2026-11-04 复核已增至 66,413 字节）只写 “When an event fires, Claude Code runs the matching hooks in parallel” 并把重复 Handler 的处理指向参考页，没有任何数字；该页本次新增的内容是 `onFailure`，见 `claude-hooks-guide-onfailure`](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/hooks#hook-handler-fields)

- [Codex Hooks 文档页（2026-11-04 以 `.md` 形式复核，48,643 字节，比 2026-10-28 记录的 47,133 字节增加）：**事件表已收录 `PreModelRequest` 与 `PostModelRequest`，全页共 14 个事件**，此前记录的「事件表仍只列 12 个、全页没有 `PreModelRequest` 或 `PostModelRequest`」与「已发布但官方文档未列」随之过期。触发时机表新增一行逐字为 “For each model request attempt | `PreModelRequest`, `PostModelRequest`”，`matcher` 支持表新增一行逐字为 “`PreModelRequest`, `PostModelRequest` | not supported | Both events run for each model request attempt”，说明段逐字为 “Use these hooks to log model requests, including retries within the same turn. `PreModelRequest` runs before each attempt. `PostModelRequest` reports the outcome. Both receive the model and `turn_id`, plus `agent_id` and `agent_type` when available. Hook output is diagnostic only. It can't change the model's context or block a request.”，`PostModelRequest` 另有 “`PostModelRequest` includes three additional fields:” 引出 `status`、`error` 与 `token_usage`；全页没有任何说明事件总数的句子。原有 12 个事件的分组仍是 “During a turn” 的 `PreToolUse`、`PermissionRequest`、`PostToolUse`、`PreCompact`、`PostCompact`、`UserPromptSubmit`、`SubagentStop`、`Stop`，“When you interrupt an active turn” 的 `Interrupt`（doesn't run for subagents），“When a session or subagent starts” 的 `SessionStart`、`SubagentStart` 与 “When the main thread ends” 的 `SessionEnd`（doesn't run for subagents）。并发口径逐字未变——“Matching hooks from multiple files all run.”、“Multiple matching command hooks for the same event are launched concurrently, so one hook can't prevent another matching hook from starting.” 与 “Codex runs up to eight background hooks concurrently per session. Additional hooks wait until a running hook finishes.”，同一节还写 “Each matching invocation runs independently, and background hooks can finish in a different order than they started.”、“When the session ends, Codex cancels unfinished background hooks and discards output that hasn't been delivered.” 与 “`SessionEnd` hooks always run synchronously.”；同步 command Hook 仍没有任何数字上限；Handler 口径仍是 “`command` and `mcp_tool` handlers are supported. `prompt` and `agent` handlers are parsed but skipped.”。失败语义在 2026-11-04 复核时仍无任何 `onFailure`、`on_failure`、`failClosed` 或同类把失败改成阻断的配置键，逐字未变的四句是 “An explicit supported denial can block an action, but a `PreToolUse` callback error, timeout, or malformed response can fail the hook without blocking the tool.”、“A hook can block an operation when the tool returns a blocking decision. Errors, missing servers, and unavailable tools don't block the operation.”、“If a command times out or exits with an error, Codex reports it as a hook failure.” 与 “Exit `0` with no output is treated as success and Codex continues.”，唯一记录的 fail-closed 仍是 “Don't return `updatedInput`, `updatedPermissions`, or `interrupt` for `PermissionRequest`; those fields are reserved for future behavior and fail closed today.”](https://learn.chatgpt.com/docs/hooks)
- [Codex 官方 Hooks 文档页（2026-11-08 复核 `https://learn.chatgpt.com/docs/hooks`，HTML 541,305 字节、去标签后正文 49,797 字符）：**没有任何文件变更观察事件**。全页 14 个事件的分组表逐字为 “During a turn | `PreToolUse`, `PermissionRequest`, `PostToolUse`, `PreCompact`, `PostCompact`, `UserPromptSubmit`, `SubagentStop`, `Stop`”、“For each model request attempt | `PreModelRequest`, `PostModelRequest`”、“When you interrupt an active turn | `Interrupt` (doesn't run for subagents)”、“When a session or subagent starts | `SessionStart`, `SubagentStart`” 与 “When the main thread ends | `SessionEnd` (doesn't run for subagents)”；全文对 `watch`、`Watch`、`watcher`、`FileChanged`、`file changed`、`CwdChanged`、`ConfigChange` 与 `watchPaths` 均为 **0 次命中**，`filesystem` 只有 3 次且都在 MCP 工具名 matcher 示例里（`mcp__filesystem__read_file`、`mcp__filesystem__.*` 与 “Match the MCP tool name, such as `mcp__filesystem__read_file`.”）。因此 Codex 侧只有按工具调用触发的路径：`PreToolUse` 逐字为 “can intercept Bash, file edits performed through `apply_patch`, MCP tool calls, and other local function tools”，`PostToolUse` 逐字为 “runs after supported tools produce output, including Bash, `apply_patch`, MCP tool calls, and other local function tools. For Bash, it also runs after commands that exit with a non-zero status. It can't undo side effects from a tool that already ran.”，两者都不覆盖 Codex 之外的进程写文件；也没有任何把路径注册进观察清单的输出字段](https://learn.chatgpt.com/docs/hooks)

- [Codex 提交 `70b3bb6d8814ab3f2045238b4ae14c43da99783b`（PR #55144 “Add observation-only model request lifecycle hooks”，2026-10-18T22:36:47Z 合入 main，改动 46 个文件）：提交说明逐字为 “Add `PreModelRequest` and `PostModelRequest` hooks around model request attempts, including retries. Pass model, session, turn, and optional subagent context; report completion, failure, or cancellation with errors and token usage when available.”、“Treat hook output as diagnostic data without allowing it to veto requests or alter model context. Wait for synchronous post-request hooks before dispatching tools, while asynchronous hooks allow streaming tool dispatch to continue.” 与 “Apply memory-consolidation hook filtering and expose the new events through configuration, protocol schemas, generated clients, and the hooks browser.”；测试说明逐字为 “Add integration coverage for failures and retries, ignored control directives, cancellation, synchronous and asynchronous tool ordering, interrupted-turn resume and token accounting, and memory-consolidation filtering. - [Codex `codex-rs/hooks/src/events/model_request.rs`（提交 `70b3bb6d8814`，5,034 字节，本次新增文件）：文件头注释逐字为 “//! Observation-only lifecycle hooks. Output is logged, never interpreted as control flow.”；`ModelRequestHookEvent` 以 `#[serde(tag = "hook_event_name")]` 分 `PreModelRequest` 与带 `status`/`error`/`token_usage` 三字段的 `PostModelRequest`，`ModelRequestStatus` 以 `#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]` 取 `Completed`/`Failed`/`Cancelled`；`ModelRequestHookInput` 的字段为 `session_id`、`turn_id`、`cwd`（`AbsolutePathBuf`）、`transcript_path`（`Option`）、`model` 与两个 `#[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]` 的 `agent_id`/`agent_type`，事件本体 `#[serde(flatten)]`；`run()` 调 `dispatcher::select_handlers(&engine.handlers, request.event.hook_event(), /*matcher_input*/ None)`，命中为空直接返回；`parse_completed` 逐字为 `let status = if result.error.is_none() && result.exit_code == Some(0) { HookRunStatus::Completed } else { HookRunStatus::Failed };`，注释 “// Observer stdout is diagnostic, including any JSON that looks like a control directive.” 之后 stdout 只进 `tracing::debug!(stdout = %result.stdout, "model request hook output")`，stderr 与执行错误各压一条 `HookOutputEntryKind::Error`，返回的 `ParsedHandler` 的 `data` 恒为 `()`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/70b3bb6d8814ab3f2045238b4ae14c43da99783b/codex-rs/hooks/src/events/model_request.rs)
- [Codex `codex-rs/hooks/schema/generated/model-request.command.input.schema.json`（提交 `70b3bb6d8814`，3,126 字节，本次新增文件）：`title` 为 `model-request.command.input`，顶层 `required` 为 `cwd`、`model`、`session_id`、`transcript_path`、`turn_id`，另有可选的 `agent_id` 与 `agent_type`；`oneOf` 两支分别是 `hook_event_name` 枚举 `PreModelRequest`（唯一必填项）与 `PostModelRequest`（`required` 为 `error`、`hook_event_name`、`status`、`token_usage`），`ModelRequestStatus` 枚举为 `completed`/`failed`/`cancelled`，`TokenUsage` 的 `required` 为 `cached_input_tokens`、`input_tokens`、`output_tokens`、`reasoning_output_tokens`、`total_tokens` 且 `cache_write_input_tokens` 默认 `0`](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/70b3bb6d8814ab3f2045238b4ae14c43da99783b/codex-rs/hooks/schema/generated/model-request.command.input.schema.json)

- [Codex `codex-rs/hooks/src/engine/dispatcher.rs`（标签 rust-v0.165.0，提交 `360319d8dee62d87e2d59fc0c21805d79519cfed`，22,340 字节）：`execute_handlers_with_metadata` 仍把每个同步 Handler 逐个 `pending.push(...)` 进同一个 `FuturesUnordered` 后统一 `await`，文件里没有条数上限、信号量或分批逻辑，`HookExecutionMode::Async` 的 Handler 走 `engine.command_runtime.schedule_async_hook(...)`，即文档里 8 个的上限只约束后台 Hook；该标签新增 `HandlerSourcePath::ExecutorScoped` 一支：这类（environment 作用域）Handler 先从同步批次里挑出放进 `executor_handlers`，等同步批次全部结束并排序后，只在 `should_stop || !should_block` 成立时用 `schedule_async_task` 逐个投递，失败只压一条 `executor-scoped hook failed` 警告，因此 environment 作用域 Hook 排在本地同步 Hook 之后且不参与阻断；`select_handlers_for_matcher_inputs` 把 `PreModelRequest` 与 `PostModelRequest` 和 `UserPromptSubmit`、`Stop`、`Interrupt` 合并成 `=> true` 的一支，配置的 `matcher` 对这些事件不做筛选](https://github.com/openai/codex/blob/360319d8dee62d87e2d59fc0c21805d79519cfed/codex-rs/hooks/src/engine/dispatcher.rs)

- [Qwen Code `docs/users/features/hooks.md`（失败与超时语义复核，2026-11-04 前移到提交 `35aa4b76ae10a458632d23c9b0052af5a31b3885`，PR #16196 “fix(core): require explicit trust for settings-file project hooks”，2026-11-03T15:54:00Z，98,620 字节，此前坐标 `d13ff87407d673c547726b43928dedba0465ffa1`）：**全页仍无 `onFailure`、`on_failure`、`failClosed` 或任何同类把失败改成阻断的配置键**。退出码表三行逐字未变——`0` “Success. A JSON object in `stdout` controls behavior. Any other `stdout`, including bare JSON values such as `42`, is plain text: it is added to the model context on `SessionStart`, `UserPromptSubmit` and `UserPromptExpansion`, and kept as a system message on other events. Output that looks like a JSON object but does not parse is never added to the model context.”、`2` “**Blocking error**. Ignores `stdout`, passes `stderr` as error feedback to the model.”、`Other` “Non-blocking error. `stderr` only shown in debug mode, execution continues.”；HTTP 侧逐字为 “HTTP hooks never follow redirects. A 3xx response is treated like any other non-2xx status: a non-blocking hook failure, and the redirect target is never contacted.”；官方 HTTP judgment adapter 示例逐字为 “Fails open on any network/timeout/malformed-response issue from the judgment backend, so an outage never blocks legitimate tool calls — same discipline the `command`-hook examples above apply with their own exit codes.”；`timeout` 单位与兼容规则逐字为 “`timeout` is in seconds for command, HTTP and prompt hooks; SDK-registered function hooks keep milliseconds.”、“Command hook timeouts used to be written in milliseconds, so for command hooks a value of `1000` or more is still read as milliseconds and existing settings keep working.” 与 “A command hook `timeout` that is not a positive number, such as `"30s"`, is ignored and the 60 second default applies.”，并有 “With debug logging enabled (`QWEN_DEBUG_LOG_FILE=1`), each command hook with a millisecond or ignored `timeout` is named once per session in that session's debug log.”；按事件硬编码的失败口径逐字为 “If the hooks fail, or do not finish within 15 seconds, the batch continues with its results unchanged.”（`PostToolBatch`）、“The turn's end (and the `Stop` hook, when it fires) waits up to 5 seconds for the final delivery to finish.” 与 “Hitting this timeout prints a warning on stderr.”（`MessageDisplay`）；事件表仍列 22 个事件。安全提醒逐字未变：“For security-sensitive hooks, a decoder failure can change fail-open or fail-closed behavior, so administrators must validate the upgraded payload against the deployed hook before rollout.”。该提交同时把设置文件里的项目 Hook 改成需要显式文件夹信任（新增 `security.folderTrust.enabled`、`security.allowedHttpHookUrls`、`security.allowPrivateNetworkHooks`、`disableAllHooks` 与 `stopHookBlockingCap` 等键的说明），属信任门禁而非失败语义、本次未处理；同批 v0.26.6/v0.26.7 发布说明里的 Mods `prompt.submit` 扩展（#15905、#16180）属 mod 事件覆盖面、本次也未处理](https://github.com/QwenLM/qwen-code/blob/35aa4b76ae10a458632d23c9b0052af5a31b3885/docs/users/features/hooks.md)
- [Qwen Code `docs/users/features/hooks.md`（提交 `1dc74d58384ca1fe8c29730970f1f7c60d1e5265`，PR #14129 “feat(core): skip re-injecting an unchanged UserPromptSubmit hook context block”，2026-10-25T14:50:26Z，96,533 字节，2026-10-28 复核执行顺序与并发）：并发口径逐字未变——“By default, hooks execute in parallel for better performance”、“Use `sequential: true` in hook definition to enforce order-dependent execution”、“Sequential hooks can modify input for subsequent hooks in the chain”、“When hooks run in parallel, they all receive the same input, the last replacement in configuration order wins, and an invalid replacement from any of them stops the call. Hooks that run sequentially each receive the previous replacement as `tool_input`.”、“When several calls from one turn are scheduled together, as in the interactive terminal UI, every call's hooks run before any of them runs.”、“A shell command that a hook could still rewrite is not run in parallel with other tool calls.”；`MessageDisplay` 一类中途 Hook 另有 “At most one mid-stream hook execution per message is in flight at a time; while one runs, newer debounced payloads _replace_ the queued one rather than piling up behind it.”；`async: true` 逐字为 “Only `command` type supports asynchronous execution. Setting `"async": true` runs the hook in the background without blocking the main flow.” 与 “Occupies one of 10 concurrent async hook slots until it finishes or reaches its `timeout` (60 seconds by default). While all 10 slots are in use, a new async hook is skipped”；`## Hook Configuration` 一节另给出一条此前未记录的来源顺序口径，逐字为 “Hooks in the system settings files (System and SystemDefaults) load with the source System and, like user hooks, regardless of folder trust. Within one event, sequential hooks from settings and extensions run in this order: Project, User, System, Extension.”；事件表仍列出 22 个事件（`PreToolUse`、`PostToolUse`、`PostToolUseFailure`、`PostToolBatch`、`UserPromptSubmit`、`UserPromptExpansion`、`SessionStart`、`SessionEnd`、`SessionDelete`、`MessageDisplay`、`Stop`、`StopFailure`、`SubagentStart`、`SubagentStop`、`PreCompact`、`PostCompact`、`Notification`、`PermissionRequest`、`PermissionDenied`、`TodoCreated`、`TodoCompleted`、`InstructionsLoaded`）；相对此前记录的提交 `52ae53b02a17d6214e9e4f81578f2ba2725539a6`（96,225 字节）的唯一差异是 `UserPromptSubmit` 的 `additionalContext` 去重句 “If the sanitized payload is byte-identical to a block a hook already delivered earlier in the same session, it is not appended again, so a deterministic standing instruction reaches the model once rather than once per turn; this is unconditional with no opt-out, and any change to the payload re-injects it.”，与并发无关](https://github.com/QwenLM/qwen-code/blob/1dc74d58384ca1fe8c29730970f1f7c60d1e5265/docs/users/features/hooks.md)
- [Qwen Code 提交 `52ae53b02a17d6214e9e4f81578f2ba2725539a6`（PR #14356 “fix(hooks): gate headless file and skill prompts before expansion”，2026-10-16 合入 main）在 `docs/users/features/hooks.md` 新增的段落逐字为 “In headless runs, an input that invokes a custom command or skill, including several stacked skills, is checked before the command expands. The hook runs once for that input, with `prompt` and `submitted_prompt` both set to the raw input, such as `/deploy staging`. A block stops the command before it runs: its `!{...}` shell commands do not run, a skill does not register its allowed tools or hooks, and no model request is made. When the input is allowed, its `additionalContext` is added once to the expanded prompt, and `UserPromptExpansion` still runs after the expansion. Hooks that the skill registers do not check the input that invoked it. Other slash commands, MCP prompts and saved workflows, and the interactive UI and ACP sessions, still run the hook on the expanded prompt.”](https://github.com/QwenLM/qwen-code/commit/52ae53b02a17d6214e9e4f81578f2ba2725539a6)
- [Qwen Code `docs/users/features/hooks.md`（标签 v0.27.0，提交 `03b0c2435be1890a21b117860eaf389076787275`，105,235 字节，2026-11-08 复核）：**事件表由 22 个增加到 23 个，新增的是 `FileChanged`**，逐字行为 “| `FileChanged` | After an explicitly watched file is added, modified, or deleted | Basename of the changed file |”；同一文档在此前固定的提交 `1dc74d58384ca1fe8c29730970f1f7c60d1e5265`（96,533 字节）与 `35aa4b76ae10a458632d23c9b0052af5a31b3885`（98,620 字节）下都是 22 个事件、`FileChanged` 与 `watchPaths` 均为 0 次命中。`### Matcher Patterns` 表新增行逐字为 “| File Events | `FileChanged` | ✅ Regex | Basename of the changed file |”，表后专段逐字为 “For `FileChanged`, registering static paths uses literal filenames in cwd; filtering events uses the basename and the rules below. See FileChanged hooks for dynamic files and recursive directories.”，即**注册用字面文件名、过滤用正则**这两件事分开。`#### FileChanged` 一节逐字为 “**Purpose**: Observes additions, modifications, and deletions of explicitly watched files in local, non-managed sessions. Changes may come from Qwen Code, shell commands, editors, or other processes. The common input fields identify the listening session, including when another session writes a shared directory.”、“**Matcher**: Filters against the file basename using the existing matcher rules. Literal cwd filenames separated by `|` also register static paths. Omitted or empty matchers, `*`, and `.*` register no static paths; they receive events from the session's watched paths, including dynamically registered paths.”，事件字段为 `{ "file_path": "/home/me/work/demo/.envrc", "event": "change" }` 加 “`file_path` is absolute. `event` is `add`, `change`, or `unlink`. The initial directory scan is not replayed, but native filesystem events from a recent write may arrive after registration. Symbolic links are not traversed; register the real directory root. Notifications are best effort after writes settle. Hook output cannot veto or undo a write or add model context.”；输出选项逐字为 “Successful synchronous `hookSpecificOutput.watchPaths`, with `hookEventName: "FileChanged"`, replaces the dynamic watch list with absolute files or recursive directories. The last valid, explicit list in hook execution order wins; `[]` clears it, while omitted or invalid lists leave it unchanged. Async output does not register paths. Static registrations remain active.”，节末另写 “Memory directories are watched only when explicitly registered; there is no dedicated auto-memory enable/disable event.”。`SessionStart` 的输出选项新增一行逐字为 “`hookSpecificOutput.watchPaths`: absolute files or recursive directories to watch for `FileChanged`, from synchronous output with `hookEventName: "SessionStart"`. See FileChanged path registration for replacement, clearing, and validation rules.”。prompt Handler 一节仍写 “Prompt hooks can be used with most hook events, including:” 后列 `PreToolUse`、`PostToolUse`、`Stop`、`SubagentStop`、`UserPromptSubmit` 五个，**没有把 `FileChanged` 列入也没有排除**，该事件能否用 prompt 与 HTTP Handler 记为未确认；退出码三档、`timeout` 秒/毫秒兼容规则、并发与顺序口径（`sequential: true`、`async: true` 占 10 个槽）与失败语义本次逐字复核未变](https://github.com/QwenLM/qwen-code/blob/03b0c2435be1890a21b117860eaf389076787275/docs/users/features/hooks.md)
- [Qwen Code `docs/users/features/file-changed-hooks.md`（标签 v0.27.0，提交 `03b0c2435be1890a21b117860eaf389076787275`，7,621 字节，`FileChanged` 专页；**该文件在标签 v0.26.9（提交 `600aff0592bcfc21668ca4aba95a5b20c6491b86`）下返回 404**，因此 v0.27.0 是首个包含它的 Release）：节首逐字为 “Use `FileChanged` to observe files changed by Qwen Code, shell commands, editors, or other processes. It runs in local, non-managed sessions.”。**输入归属**逐字为 “The common `session_id` and `cwd` belong to the listening session; they do not identify the writer. Multiple sessions watching a shared directory can each receive its changes. File content is not included.”，另写 “Only file events are delivered. Directory-self `addDir` and `unlinkDir` events are not delivered. Integrations tracking a watched root's own lifecycle must check filesystem state (for example with `stat`) and reconcile independently; a child's `unlink` does not prove that the root was removed.”。**静态注册**逐字为 “Add a `FileChanged` hook with literal filenames separated by `|`:”（示例 `"matcher": ".envrc|.env"`，命令用 `$QWEN_PROJECT_DIR`）、“This registers `.envrc` and `.env` in the session's cwd. Static registration does not expand globs or scan directories recursively.”、“Regex patterns filter already watched paths; register their files or directories explicitly through `watchPaths`.” 与 “Static roots accept only bare literal filenames, including dotfiles. Regex/glob metacharacters and path separators do not create static roots. Existing directories and paths that cannot be inspected are excluded; missing files may be watched for creation. Static file discovery observes their parent directory while excluding unrelated entries before traversal. Use explicit `watchPaths` for recursive directories. Changing the session working directory relocates static roots before relocation finishes; explicit absolute dynamic roots stay unchanged.”。**动态注册**逐字为 “A successful synchronous `SessionStart` hook can register absolute files or directories through `hookSpecificOutput.watchPaths`. Directories are watched recursively, including children created later. Recursive discovery skips `.git` and `node_modules` subtrees. To observe a path inside them, register that file or directory explicitly. Special file types are ignored, and ordinary directories have no depth limit. Initial registration waits for watcher readiness before `SessionStart` returns.”，另有 “Recursive discovery ignores atomic-write temporary files named `<target>.<12 lowercase hex characters>.tmp`, including delayed renames and orphans. Explicitly registering such a file still observes it. Directories with this suffix remain traversable, and other `.tmp` files are observed normally.”。**替换语义表**四行逐字为 “A list of absolute files or directories | Replaces the previous dynamic list”、“`[]` | Clears the dynamic list”、“Omitted or invalid `watchPaths` | Keeps the previous dynamic list”、“Output from an async hook | Does not register or change paths”，并写 “When several hooks return lists, the last valid, explicitly supplied list in hook execution order wins. All entries must be absolute paths; one invalid entry makes the list invalid. Static filename registrations remain active after a dynamic replacement or clear.”。**多工作区与远程边界**逐字为 “For several workspaces in the same filesystem, register all their actual memory directories in one list. Route notifications using `file_path` and your own directory-to-workspace mapping, rather than the listening session's `cwd`. Independent sessions can observe the same shared root, so deduplicate the work in the business handler. Roots in another container or remote instance need a watcher in that filesystem owner; registering their paths on the host does not observe them.”。**观察限制**逐字为 “The initial directory scan is not replayed. Native filesystem events from a recent write may arrive after registration. Explicitly registered symbolic-link files and directory roots observe their targets, including links in ancestor paths. Notifications use the configured alias; registering multiple aliases to one target produces a notification for each alias. Refresh registration after retargeting a link. Symbolic links discovered inside a recursive root are not traversed. Events are reported after writes settle.”、“Watching is best effort: filesystem coalescing, inaccessible paths, process downtime, and path reconfiguration can leave gaps. Integrations that require a complete synchronized copy must handle initial scans, reconciliation, and retry.”、“Watcher or hook failures are logged and leave the originating file operation unchanged.” 与 “`FileChanged` cannot veto or undo a write or add model context. Only explicitly registered paths are watched: memory folders are not registered automatically, and there is no dedicated auto-memory enable/disable event. A hook that writes a watched file can trigger itself; avoid those writes or deduplicate the work.”。**Surface 条件**逐字为 “SSH execution environments, shell sandbox workers, and managed dispatchers do not start a local filesystem watcher. Normal hook enablement and trust rules still apply. Watching stops when its owning session shuts down.”。官方给的示例用途是把 `/memory` 显示的记忆目录注册进观察清单，并提醒 “Choose a log or synchronization destination outside the watched paths to avoid triggering the handler with its own writes.”](https://github.com/QwenLM/qwen-code/blob/03b0c2435be1890a21b117860eaf389076787275/docs/users/features/file-changed-hooks.md)
- [Qwen Code `packages/core/src/hooks/file-changed-watcher.ts`（标签 v0.27.0，提交 `03b0c2435be1890a21b117860eaf389076787275`，9,585 字节；**同一文件在标签 v0.26.9 下返回 404**）：实现基于 `import { watch, type FSWatcher } from 'chokidar'`，`watch(watchRoots, {...})` 的选项逐字为 `ignoreInitial: true`（对应文档 “The initial directory scan is not replayed”）、`followSymlinks: false`（对应 “Symbolic links discovered inside a recursive root are not traversed”）与 **`awaitWriteFinish: { stabilityThreshold: 200, pollInterval: 50 }`**，即文档只写 “Events are reported after writes settle” 的那个「settle」在源码里是**写入后稳定 200 毫秒、每 50 毫秒轮询一次**，官方文档没有给出这两个数字。`ignored` 回调逐字包含 `if (stats && !stats.isFile() && !stats.isDirectory()) return true;`（对应 “Special file types are ignored”）、原子写临时文件判断 `/\.[0-9a-f]{12}\.tmp$/.test(path.basename(file)) && !observed.includes(file)`（对应 “atomic-write temporary files named `<target>.<12 lowercase hex characters>.tmp`”，且显式注册的同名文件仍被观察）、以及动态根内的 `.git`／`node_modules` 跳过 `!path.relative(root, file).split(path.sep).some((part) => part === '.git' || part === 'node_modules')`；**没有传 `depth` 选项**，对应文档 “ordinary directories have no depth limit”。静态目标另有一条只允许目录的判断 `if (staticTargets.has(file)) return stats?.isDirectory() ?? false;` 与注释 “// Only ancestors of requested paths may lead discovery downward.”；调试通道为 `createDebugLogger('FILE_CHANGED_WATCHER')`，清理走 `closeFileWatcher`（`../utils/file-watcher-cleanup.js`），事件类型来自 `./types.js` 的 `FileChangeEvent`](https://github.com/QwenLM/qwen-code/blob/03b0c2435be1890a21b117860eaf389076787275/packages/core/src/hooks/file-changed-watcher.ts)
- [Qwen Code Release v0.27.0（2026-11-07T20:45:25Z 发布，稳定通道，标签提交 `03b0c2435be1890a21b117860eaf389076787275`）：Complete Change List 的 Features 首条逐字为 “Added FileChanged hooks for local non-managed sessions, reporting add, change, and unlink events for watched files and directories. - [Kimi Code 中文 Hooks 文档 `docs/zh/customization/hooks.md`（fail-open 与超时语义复核，2026-11-04 前移到提交 `5f3ab5fac81e050fc2fa10787dd560655f377d16`，2026-10-29T12:43:07Z，9,523 字节，此前坐标 `494df61ce9858c70a9f37a996614534bc9b1cd12` 下同一文件为 8,319 字节）：**新增一整段失败语义**，逐字为 “当 hook 执行失败时（无法启动、超时或以错误退出码结束），CLI 会记录日志，并在会话中显示一次警告；之后的失败只记录日志。阻断决策保持 fail-open：失败的 hook 永远不会阻断操作。”——该段在历史坐标 `494df61ce985` 下不存在，**据此确认「命令无法启动」被官方归入失败形态之一且仍按 fail-open 处理**，此前记录的「文档没有单独描述命令无法启动的情形、启动失败属于哪种取值记为未确认」随之过期。退出码表四行逐字为 `0`「正常结束，放行」→「继续执行，若标准输出（stdout）有内容可附加到上下文」、`2`「主动阻断」→「停止当前操作；错误输出（stderr，`console.error` 打印的内容）作为阻断原因」、`其他非零值`「脚本出错」→「默认放行（fail-open）」、`超时或崩溃`「脚本异常」→「默认放行（fail-open）」；命名与边界两句逐字为 “即使脚本报错或超时，CLI 也**不会因此中断你的工作**。这种"出错就放行"的设计称为 fail-open（失败开放），避免 hook 异常阻塞主流程。” 与 “正因为 fail-open，Hooks 适合做提醒和轻量拦截，但**不应作为唯一的安全防线**。对真正高风险的操作，仍需依赖权限审批和人工确认。”；`timeout` 字段行逐字为 “`timeout` | `integer` | 否 | 超时秒数，范围 1–600；默认 30 秒”，超时处置逐字为 “非 Windows 平台上，hook 进程运行在独立进程组中；超时后 CLI 先发送信号让脚本有机会善后，再强制终止。”；可阻断与观察型事件的分界逐字为 “只有**可阻断事件**（`PreToolUse`、`Stop`、`UserPromptSubmit`）的返回值会影响主流程。其余事件属于**观察型事件**：触发后即发即忘，不管脚本返回什么，主流程都不会改变。”；配置面逐字为 “`[[hooks]]` 只允许这四个字段，多写会导致配置文件加载失败。”，四字段即 `event`、`matcher`、`command`、`timeout`，**因此写进 `onFailure` 一类键会直接让配置文件加载失败**；全页 `onFailure`、`sequential` 与任何并发数字上限均未出现](https://github.com/MoonshotAI/kimi-code/blob/5f3ab5fac81e050fc2fa10787dd560655f377d16/docs/zh/customization/hooks.md)
- [Kimi Code 提交 `c1459a61845056832b85a0a220ee11a2bc5132f8`（PR #4242 “fix(server): run agent hooks in the session's working directory”，2026-10-15T11:38:24Z 合入 main）：`AgentExternalHooksService` 的 `fireAndForgetTrigger(event)`（事件总线触发的 Hook）与 `PreToolUse`／`UserPromptSubmit`／`Stop`／`PreCompact` 四类 Agent 级 Hook 改为显式传入 `cwd: sessionContext.cwd`；此前这些 Agent 级 Hook 没有传 `cwd`、Hook 运行器回落到 `bootstrap.cwd`，因而在引擎或服务器进程的启动目录里执行，与会话级 Hook 已经在会话目录执行不一致；测试把引擎启动目录设为 `/launch-dir` 并断言 Agent 级 Hook 拿到会话目录 `/tmp`，`fullCompaction.test.ts` 另断言 `PreCompact`／`PostCompact` 载荷里的 `cwd` 是会话目录。附带的 changeset 文件为 `.changeset/hooks-session-cwd.md`，对 `@moonshot-ai/kimi-code` 与 `@moonshot-ai/kimi-code-sdk` 都是 patch；该修正已随 Release `@moonshot-ai/kimi-code@2.3.0`（2026-10-21T05:51:43Z）发布，此前记录的「核对日期最新 Release 是 2.2.0、尚未发布」随之过期；官方中文 Hooks 文档在核对日期仍没有提到服务器路径曾有这一差异](https://github.com/MoonshotAI/kimi-code/commit/c1459a61845056832b85a0a220ee11a2bc5132f8)
- [Kimi Code `apps/kimi-code/CHANGELOG.md`（固定到提交 `4bad441fef34a6733f5c065ddea6c1a9c1c28e50`，“chore: sync release/2026-10-21 (2.3.0) back to main (#4306)”，431,837 字节）：`## 2.3.0` 的 Patch Changes 里逐字列出 “[#4242](https://github.com/MoonshotAI/kimi-code/pull/4242) [`c1459a6`](https://github.com/MoonshotAI/kimi-code/commit/c1459a61845056832b85a0a220ee11a2bc5132f8) Thanks [@wbxl2000](https://github.com/wbxl2000)! - Run hooks such as `PreToolUse` and `Stop` in the session's project directory, as documented, instead of the directory the server was started from.”，Release `@moonshot-ai/kimi-code@2.3.0` 于 2026-10-21T05:51:43Z 发布、发布说明正文含同一条；2.3.0 与核对日期最新的 2.4.0（2026-10-28T11:45:13Z）两版都没有任何关于同一事件并发数或 `sequential` 顺序执行的条目](https://github.com/MoonshotAI/kimi-code/blob/4bad441fef34a6733f5c065ddea6c1a9c1c28e50/apps/kimi-code/CHANGELOG.md)
- [Kimi Code `docs/zh/customization/hooks.md`（标签 `@moonshot-ai/kimi-code@2.5.2`，提交 `1c142a007c10e881602690def96057c23a2baa12`，9,523 字节，2026-11-08 复核）：**没有任何文件变更观察事件**。`## 事件一览` 表共 20 行，逐字为 `UserPromptSubmit`、`UserPromptQueued`、`PreToolUse`、`Stop`、`TurnStarted`、`PostToolUse`、`PostToolUseFailure`、`PermissionRequest`、`PermissionResult`、`SessionStart`、`SessionEnd`、`SessionHeartbeat`、`SubagentStart`、`SubagentStop`、`TaskStarted`、`StopFailure`、`Interrupt`、`PreCompact`、`PostCompact` 与 `Notification`；全文对 `watch`、`FileChanged`、`监听`、`变更` 均为 **0 次命中**，`文件` 只有 3 次、没有一次指文件系统观察——两处说明 Hook 的执行位置（“钩子始终在运行 Kimi Code 的机器上执行，因此在远程会话中它们读到的是本机的文件和进程，而非目标环境的。”、“没有项目级 hook 文件，因此远程目标上检出的内容永远无法向你的会话注入 hook 定义。”），一处是配置面的 “`[[hooks]]` 只允许这四个字段，多写会导致配置文件加载失败。”；写成 `变化` 的 2 次都指 `Notification` 的 “后台任务状态变化时触发”。唯一按时间触发的事件是 `SessionHeartbeat`，逐字为 “会话存活期间每 60 秒触发一次，仅配置本事件时计时器才运行；payload 含 `uptime_ms`”，与文件系统无关；可阻断事件仍只有 `PreToolUse`、`Stop`、`UserPromptSubmit` 三个，其余 “触发后即发即忘”。配置面也不给注册观察路径的余地：`[[hooks]]` 只允许 `event`、`matcher`、`command`、`timeout` 四个字段、多写会导致配置文件加载失败，因此没有 `watchPaths` 一类输出键的容身之处](https://github.com/MoonshotAI/kimi-code/blob/1c142a007c10e881602690def96057c23a2baa12/docs/zh/customization/hooks.md)

- [Qoder CLI Hooks 页（失败与超时语义复核，2026-11-04，规范地址已改为 `https://docs.qoder.com/cli/hooks`，`en/cli/hooks` 会跳过去）：**全页没有 `onFailure`、`on_failure`、`failClosed` 或任何把失败改成阻断的配置键，阻断只能靠退出码 2**。退出码三档逐字为 “Hooks control behavior through exit codes and stdout.”、“`0`: success; stdout is parsed according to the rules below.”、“`2`: blocking; stderr content is fed back to the Agent (only effective for events that support blocking).” 与 “Other values: non-blocking error; stdout is ignored, stderr is written to diagnostic logs, the main flow continues.”，JSON 侧另有 “`decision` … `allow` or `deny`, event-specific decision; `deny` is equivalent to exit 2.”；四类 Handler 的 `timeout` 逐字为 command “Timeout in seconds, default 600”、http “Timeout in seconds, default 600”、prompt “Timeout in seconds, default 30”、agent “Timeout in seconds, default 60”。页面**仍然没有描述 Hook 命令启动不了（例如可执行文件缺失）或超过 `timeout` 之后的具体后果**，这两点继续记为未确认。按事件硬编码的例外逐字为 `WorktreeCreate` “The hook must return the absolute path of the worktree; any non-zero exit code is treated as failure.”、`ConfigChange` “Blocking: exit 2 prevents the change from being applied to the current session. Exception: when source is policy_settings, hooks still fire for audit purposes but the change is enforced and cannot be blocked.”、`StopFailure` 与 `InstructionsLoaded` 各写 “Notification only — output and exit code are ignored.”、`WorktreeRemove` “Notification only — failures are surfaced via stderr.”；可阻断事件的效果分别是 `UserPromptSubmit` “Blocking: exit 2 rejects the prompt; stderr is shown to the user.”、`PreToolUse` “Blocking: exit 2; stderr is returned to the Agent as an error.”、`Stop` “Blocking: exit 2; stderr is injected into the conversation as a message and the Agent continues working.”、`SubagentStop` “Blocking: exit 2; stderr is injected into the sub-agent's conversation.”、`PreCompact` “Blocking: exit 2 prevents this compaction.”、`Elicitation` “Blocking: exit 2 declines the elicitation.” 与 `ElicitationResult` “Blocking: exit 2 rewrites action to decline.”；后台执行的 `asyncRewake` 逐字为 “When `true`, runs in the background; if it exits with code 2, the CLI builds a system reminder from stderr/stdout/error and wakes the model — useful for long-running checks”，**即后台 Hook 的退出码 2 不阻断任何动作、改成唤醒模型**](https://docs.qoder.com/cli/hooks)
- [Qoder CLI Hooks 页的 “Working Directory and Files” 分组（2026-11-08 复核 `https://docs.qoder.com/cli/hooks`，HTML 1,268,339 字节、去标签后正文 32,243 字符）：`FileChanged` 与 `CwdChanged` 此前只作为事件名出现在本仓库记录的 23 个事件清单里，本次补录其字段与注册方式。事件总览表对应两行逐字为 “| CwdChanged | — | — | old_cwd, new_cwd |” 与 “| FileChanged | filename basename | — | file_path, event |”，即 `FileChanged` 的 matcher 匹配文件名 basename、**退出码 2 不阻断**。`FileChanged` 一节逐字为 “Triggered when a watched file changes.”、“**matcher field:** Basename of the changed file (supports exact match, `|` multi-value, regex)”、附加输入字段 `{ "file_path": "/abs/path/file.ts", "event": "change" }` 加 “`event` values: `change` / `add` / `unlink`.”、输出为 “**hookSpecificOutput:** `additionalContext`, `watchPaths` (same as CwdChanged)”。`watchPaths` 的定义在 `CwdChanged` 一节，逐字为 “Absolute paths to register with the `FileChanged` watcher”，同节给出 `{ "old_cwd": "/old", "new_cwd": "/new" }` 与 `additionalContext` 的 “Context injected into the conversation”。**全页 `watchPaths` 只出现 2 次、`watcher` 与 `watched` 各 1 次，都在上面这两节里**：`SessionStart` 的 `hookSpecificOutput` 逐字只有 “`additionalContext` (context injected into the conversation)” 加一句 “When the hook returns plain text (not JSON), the stdout is also injected into the conversation as context.”，**没有 `watchPaths`**，`UserPromptSubmit` 的是 `additionalContext` 与 `sessionTitle`，因此 Qoder 只有 `CwdChanged` 与 `FileChanged` 两个事件能注册观察路径。页面没有说明 watcher 的实现、递归与否、静态文件名注册、初始观察清单从哪来（既然 `SessionStart` 不能注册、`FileChanged` 又要先有被观察文件才会触发）、写入稳定判定、符号链接处理或哪些 Surface 不启动 watcher，这几点全部记为未确认；也没有给出观察路径数量或目录深度上限](https://docs.qoder.com/cli/hooks)
- [Qoder CLI Hooks 页（2026-10-28 复核执行顺序与并发，HTML 1,601,368 字节、去标签后正文 33,169 字符）：全页仍没有任何说明匹配同一事件的多个 Hook 是并行还是顺序执行的语句，也没有数字形式的并发上限，全页连 “batch” 一词都没有出现，与执行顺序相关的只有配置合并口径 “All three sources are **loaded and merged together** (hooks for the same event do not override each other)”；事件表仍是 23 个（`SessionStart`、`SessionEnd`、`UserPromptSubmit`、`PreToolUse`、`PostToolUse`、`PostToolUseFailure`、`PermissionRequest`、`PermissionDenied`、`Stop`、`StopFailure`、`SubagentStart`、`SubagentStop`、`PreCompact`、`PostCompact`、`Notification`、`InstructionsLoaded`、`ConfigChange`、`CwdChanged`、`FileChanged`、`WorktreeCreate`、`WorktreeRemove`、`Elicitation`、`ElicitationResult`）；`timeout` 默认值仍是 command 600、http 600、prompt 30、agent 60；后台执行仍只有组级 `async`（“When `true`, all hooks in the group run in the background without blocking the current operation; results are injected as additional context in the next model turn”）、条目级 `async`（“When `true`, this single hook runs in the background; overrides the group-level `async`”）与 `asyncRewake`（“When `true`, runs in the background; if it exits with code 2, the CLI builds a system reminder from stderr/stdout/error and wakes the model — useful for long-running checks”），三者都没有并发数量限制](https://docs.qoder.com/en/cli/hooks)
- [Qoder CLI Release Notes（2026-11-04 复核 Hook 相关条目，HTML 1,419,034 字节）：最新条目为 **CLI 1.1.82（November 3, 2026，“Session status reporting via OSC 7501 and reconnection fixes”）**，其前的 1.1.81（October 29, 2026，“Introducing CodeMode and More Reliable Session Continuity”）；这两版都没有任何提到 Hook、退出码、超时、信任或阻断的条目，因此 Hooks 页没有 `onFailure` 一类失败开关这条结论在发行说明侧也没有反证。仍是最新 Hook 条目的三版为 1.1.80（October 27, 2026，“Performance improvements and Auto Mode enhancements”，四条里没有 Hook 条目）、1.1.79（October 26, 2026）的 “Fixed Hook progress from background Agent incorrectly showing in the TUI” 与 1.1.78（October 23, 2026，标题 “Hook Reliability Improvements and Safer File Encoding Handling”）的两条 “Fixed SessionStart Hook context being lost after compaction”、“Added display of Hook batch progress and waiting time”；1.1.77（October 22, 2026）、1.1.76（October 21, 2026）、1.1.75（October 20, 2026）与 1.1.74（October 19, 2026）四版没有任何 Hook 条目。“Hook batch progress and waiting time” 只在 Release Notes 出现，Hooks 页没有 batch 的定义、也没有说明一个 batch 内的 Hook 是并行还是顺序执行，因此同事件的执行顺序与并发数仍记为未确认](https://docs.qoder.com/release-notes/qoder-cli)
- [Qoder CLI 官方更新日志（2026-11-08 复核 `https://changelog.qoder.com/cli`，HTML 782,358 字节、去标签后正文 69,863 字符；`https://docs.qoder.com/release-notes/qoder-cli` 仍以 307 跳转到该址）：全文对 `FileChanged`、`watchPaths`、`watcher` 与 `CwdChanged` 均为 **0 次命中**，因此无法给 `FileChanged` 事件定出发行版坐标。最新三条分别是 **CLI 1.1.84（November 6, 2026，“Improves Session Compaction and MCP Status Display”）**、**CLI 1.1.83（November 4, 2026，“Session Reliability Improvements and Enterprise Mode Controls”）** 与 CLI 1.1.82（November 3, 2026）；1.1.83 的条目逐字为 “The built-in `/verify` skill now requires explicit user invocation and can no longer be triggered automatically by the model”、“Fixed an error when resuming very large sessions with `/resume`”、“Added support for enterprise admins to disable Auto and YOLO modes”、“Fixed Windows installation corrupting Chinese directory names in the user PATH or adding duplicate Qoder CLI paths when using a non-UTF-8 code page”、“Improved retry logic so that after a successful retry of an oversized request, a subsequent oversized request can be retried again”、“Improved change detection for read files to prevent compaction from being triggered directly when content reaches the limit without any conversation” 与 “Fixed an issue where retrying in the same session could not resume writing conversation history after a file-busy co…”（页面文本在此截断），其中 “change detection for read files” 指读取内容的变更检测、与 Hook 的文件观察无关；1.1.84 的两条是 “Improved retention of recent responses and tool results so they are no longer lost after restarting and resuming a session” 与 “Fixed services pending authentication not correctly displaying their status in the `/mcp` command interface list”。仍是最新 Hook 条目的三版依旧为 1.1.80、1.1.79 与 1.1.78](https://changelog.qoder.com/cli)
